> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Ihe Nzuzo Ndị A Chọrọ

A na-emepụta ihe nzuzo ndị edoghị nke akara ha bụ **Ee** na-akpaghị aka n’oge mbido mbụ (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) ma chekwaa ha na `server.env` n’ime ndekọ data — debe ha n’onwe gị mgbe ndekọ ahụ bụ nke na-adịru nwa oge. Ihe dị iche bụ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: anaghị echekwa ya na `server.env` — ebe mbido sava na-emepụta uru nwa oge pụrụ iche maka mbido ọ bụla nke naanị bridge dị n’ime usoro ahụ nwere ike iji, ya mere ndị ahịa bridge mpụga ga-edobe ya n’onwe ha.

| Agbanwe                      | Achọrọ            | Ndabara                            | Faịlụ Isi                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**            | _(ọ dịghị)_                        | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-eji mbinye aka akwado/nyochaa kuki nnọkọ dashboard niile (JWT). Jiri `openssl rand -base64 48` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**            | _(a na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/apiKey.ts`                              | Ihe nzuzo a na-eji emepụta ma nyochaa checksum nke igodo API ejikọrọ na igwe. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, usoro mmalite nkịtị na-emepụta ya ma chekwaa ya na `server.env` ma ọ bụrụ na enwere ike ide n'ime faịlụ ahụ. Debe ya kpọmkwem ma ọ bụrụ na ndekọ data bụ nke nwa oge. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Na-edobe okwuntughe dashboard n’oge mbido ma ọ bụrụ na echekwabeghị nke ọ bụla; a na-eleghara ya anya ozugbo echekwara okwuntughe. Nwụnye npm na nke sitere na source na-edepụta `.env.example` gaa na `.env`, ya mere ha na-amalite site na placeholder `CHANGEME` nke ọha maara: dochie ya tupu mbido mbụ, ma ọ bụ debe `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (efu) n’ime `.env` ahụ iji mepụta okwuntughe n’ime onboarding wizard n’oge nleta mbụ, dịka uru edoghị (ndabara Docker image) si eme. Gbanwee okwuntughe echekwara na Dashboard → Ntọala → Nchekwa, ma ọ bụ mee `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` n’ime source checkout) ebe edobere `DATA_DIR` ka ọ bụrụ ndekọ data sava ahụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (mmepụta)  | _(edoghị)_                         | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Ihe nzuzo nkekọrịta maka bridge WebSocket nke Codex Responses dị n’ime. Ọ na-enyocha arịrịọ bridge n’etiti relay WS nke Electron/ihe nchọgharị na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **A CHỌRỌ YA na mmepụta — mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ajụ arịrịọ bridge WS niile.** Jiri `openssl rand -base64 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba               | _(git SHA)_                        | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | ID doro anya maka ime ka cache service-worker ghara ịdị ochie n’ime shell PWA (#11779); ọ bụ nke mbụ n’usoro mkpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ n’usoro ahụ — ndị na-ewu PaaS (dịka ụdị Heroku) na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ commit e tinyere n’ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba               | _(ewepụtara)_                      | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke oge owuwu nke onye ahịa na-eji edebanye `/sw.js?v=…`; a na-ewepụta ya site na abụọ dị n’elu, emesịa git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaghị aka) | _(akpaghị aka n’oge mbido ọ bụla)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Ihe nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla nke na-egosi na stampụ peer-IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute n’onwe ya (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-atụkwasị mpaghara arịrịọ obi (mgbochi loopback/LAN nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe stampụ ahụ bu token a. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka n’oge mbido ọ bụla — hapụ ya ka edoghị ya; naanị debe ya kpọmkwem maka nhazi nwere ọtụtụ usoro ndị ga-ekekọrịta stampụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Mba               | _(edoghị)_                         | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IP ma ọ bụ oke CIDR nke reverse proxy ndị e ji rịkọm kewaa, nke ndepụta ikike/mmachibido IP nwere ike ịtụkwasị `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` ha obi, tinyere loopback, adreesị netwọkụ onwe na nsọtụ Cloudflare, nke a na-atụkwasị obi mgbe niile. A chọrọ ya naanị maka proxy dị na adreesị ọha ọzọ ọ bụla; ma ọ bụghị ya, adreesị proxy ahụ n’onwe ya ka nzacha ahụ na-enyocha. Throttle onye ahịa ọ bụla nke relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) na-eji otu adreesị onye ahịa ahụ e kpebisiri ike dịka igodo, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-adabere naanị na headers forwarding.                                                                                                                      |

### Iwu Mepụta

```bash
# Mepụta ihe nzuzo anọ ahụ niile n’otu oge:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Etinyela faịlụ `.env` nwere ezi ihe nzuzo na njikwa ụdị. `.gitignore` ewepụlarị `.env`, mana nyochaa ya tupu ịkwanye.

---

## 2. Nchekwa & Ebe Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa data niile na-adịgide adịgide. Ngbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izochi ya, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Ngbanwe                                    | Ndabara                           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`                   | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka DB SQLite, nkwado ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka volume Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(unset)_                         | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka ihe nche test/eval nke DATA_DIR (#10428). A na-ebugharị nnwale na nyocha eval/print nke Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) ndị na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na ndekọ temp a ga-atụfu, ka ha ghara imepe ebe nchekwa data n'ezie nke onye nchịkwa; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji họrọ ịlaghachi n'iji ndekọ n'ezie ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(unset)_                         | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-arụ ọrụ. `npm run build:release` na-etinye ya; enwere ike itinye ya n'ime container ndị na-enweghị sentinel `dist/BUILD_SHA`. A na-egosi ya dị ka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                     | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke ọnụ ụzọ provenance pack-artifact ji atụnyere build SHA (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(unset)_                         | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka e kwakọba build nke SHA ya na-adịghị n'ahịrị release, na-edekọ ya dị ka canary e kpachapụrụ anya kama ime ka ọnụ ụzọ ahụ daa (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(unset)_                         | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Igodo API maka nyocha smoke nke mbugharị canary, nke a na-eziga dị ka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị script mbugharị na-eji ya (#10429), server anaghị eji ya. Ọ nweghị njikọ na ngbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke ngwa nnwale smoke CLI a ga-ahọrọ isonye na ya (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` n'ime `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnyocha smoke n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(unset)_                         | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Mgbama oge build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, worker build ọ bụla e mepụtara na-eketakwa ya, ka oyi akwa DB weghachite stub na-adịghị eme ihe kama ibunye addon better-sqlite3 nke native (nke na-akwụsị worker mgbe ọ na-apụ). E kwesịghị ịtọ ya maka server na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                               | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha prebuild nke dependency native nhọrọ maka osisi vendored pụrụ iche. Nke a anaghị eme ka dependency na-efu nwee ike ịrụ build; jiri ya naanị mgbe a na-enye dependency native site n'ụzọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(unset)_                         | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Aha ọzọ ndabere** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe `DATA_DIR` edoghị. A na-eji ya achọta cache session thread-sticky nke executor PromptQL dị na disk (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edoghị ngbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ ga-anọ naanị na memory (agaghị echekwa ya n'oge restart).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(unset)_                         | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Ndekọ nke **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — bụrụkwa mgbọrọgwụ ebe plugin manager na-etinye ihe — na-anọchi ndabara sitere na home (#11827). Tụnye ya aka na osisi plugin e ji bind-mount tinye na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji gbanwee ebe ụzọ scan dị (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile metụtara home). Mgbe edoghị ya = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe process ahụ anaghị ebupụ home ọ bụla ma ọlị — ya bụ, enweghị nchọpụta na-enweghị ọkwa nke ngbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-edekọ ndekọ e kpebiri otu ugboro mgbe mmalite dị ka `scanner.dir_resolved`, tinyere input meriri. N'akụkụ server naanị: plugin iwu CLI na-ejigide `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo = agbanyụrụ ya)_ | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka izo nchekwa data SQLite niile e zoro mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                              | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Akara ụdị maka igodo izo ya ezo. Mee ka ọnụọgụ ya bawanye mgbe ị na-atụgharị igodo iji kwado ịtọghe nkwado ndabere ochie e zoro ezo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                           | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amafe nkwado ndabere faịlụ SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile/tupu ide ihe (ichekwa/ihichapụ ọnụahịa models.dev, ide ntọala). Nkwado ndabere nke aka na nke tupu iweghachi ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ **naghị** agbanyụ snapshot nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke onye na-eme migration ga-emerịrị ma ọ bụ ihe mgbochi mass-migration maka DB na-adịgide adịgide dị adị. A na-amachi nkwado ndabere ndị na-abụghị nke aka ka ha ghara ịkarị otu ugboro n'ime nkeji 60. **Settings → Storage** nke Dashboard nwere ike ịgbanyụ nkwado ndabere akpaka a na-eme mgbe niile n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(edoghị/agbanyụrụ)_              | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Millisekọnd; ụkpụrụ ndị na-erughị 1 ma ọ bụ ndị na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-anọgide na-agbanyụghị; ọ na-adọ aka ná ntị naanị otu ugboro n'ime 10s; ahịrị ndekọ nwere naanị driver, operation, na elapsedMs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(edoghị)_                        | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha ọzọ ochie** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dị ka nhọrọ ndabere mgbe mgbanwe bụ isi adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(edoghị)_                        | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | A na-anabata **aha ọzọ ochie** (ụdị e jiri Base64 koodu mee) dị ka nhọrọ ndabere. A na-akọwapụta koodu ya na-akpaghị aka tupu eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(edoghị)_                        | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Dochie oge dị n'etiti nyocha ahụike SQLite nke a na-eme kwa oge (ms). Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-enweta ụkpụrụ ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(ewepụla ya)_                    | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Ewepụla ya.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` a na-eme kwa oge mgbe sistemụ na-arụ ọrụ nwere ike imebi nkekọ wal-index nke a na-ekekọrịta ma mee ka usoro ahụ daa site na SIGBUS (#13973), ya mere scheduler ahụ adịghịzi. Mgbanwe ahụ anaghị arụ ọrụ: ụkpụrụ dị mma na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro na e kwụsịla iji ya, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ mgbe edoghị ya na-anọ nkịtị. A na-elekọta WAL site na PASSIVE checkpoints (n'okpuru), a na-ebipụkwa ya site na shutdown checkpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                     | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Dochie oge dị n'etiti `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` ndị a na-eme ugboro ugboro (ms). Ọ na-eme ka frame WAL ndị na-echere dị obere ka checkpoints wee nọgide na-adị ngwa ngwa, faịlụ WAL agaghịkwa agafe oke n'etiti mbipụ ndị a na-eme mgbe a na-emechi sistemụ. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                             | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe tick PASSIVE chọpụtara na faịlụ WAL agafeela nha a, mee `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL wee malite ọzọ n'edeghịgharị wal-index e jikọtara. Ewepụla checkpoints nke truncate-mode mgbe sistemụ na-arụ ọrụ (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                           | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji kwụsị usoro ahụ mgbe nrụgide akụrụngwa dị oke njọ nọgidere ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, nke ga-enye onye nlekọta (systemd `Restart=always`, iwu mmalitegharị Docker) ohere iweghachite usoro dị ọcha kama ịnọgide na-enye 503s ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                     | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke njọ ga-adịgide tupu nkwụsị maka mmalitegharị onwe ya amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                           | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji leghara `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) nke host dum anya mgbe agbata obi na-eme mkpọtụ kpalitere 503s ebe usoro a dị mma (#13124). Ihe nche cgroup na V8 heap ga-anọgide na-arụ ọrụ. PSI ga-akwụsịkwa ịgụta dị ka ihe akaebe kernel throttle maka `cgroup_high`: ngụkọta cgroup karịrị `memory.high` ga-abụzi nke dị oke njọ naanị mgbe `memory.events` `high`/`max` bawanyere. Ma ọ bụrụ na `memory.events` adịghị, a ga-edowe raw ratio ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(nchọpụta akpaka)_               | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ụzọ doro anya (zuru ezu ma ọ bụ nke dabere na cwd) gaa na `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe a na-eji `sql.js` WASM fallback adapter. A na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na package dependencies na nhazi ndị nwere ike ịdị mgbe edoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                              | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edobe checkpoints, faịlụ input/output/error ndị e zoro aka na ha, na row nke ọrụ Batch API kwụsịrị (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) tupu usoro nhicha akpaka ehichapụ ha. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ dabara na oge OpenAI ji edobe output Batch API nke ya. Ọ naghị emetụta route `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` nke operator na-akpalite, nke na-anọgide na-arụ ọrụ n'enweghị ọnọdụ ọ bụla (enweghị age filter) dịka e siri hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                           | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, kwe ka usoro nhicha akpaka hichapụ ọrụ Batch API kwụsịrị (na checkpoints ha) ndị gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ma kpochapụkwa ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ ndị ebulitere mgbe ha gafere `expires_at` nke ha. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: install ọ bụla dị ugbu a ga-edobe data a kpọmkwem dịka ọ dị na mbụ ruo mgbe operator họọrọ ịgbanye ya. Ọ bụkwa feature flag enwere ike idezi na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere DB healthcheck kpamkpam n'oge startup. Ọ bara uru maka task ndị na-adị obere oge na integration tests.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye loop DB healthcheck ka ọ gbanye, ọbụna mgbe a gaara amafere ya n'ọnọdụ nkịtị (dịka task ndị na-adị obere oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                               | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere native-runtime warm-up n'oge `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na CI/headless installs ebe e wurula sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(chọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_        | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Dochie directory nke migration runner na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ebunye migrations agbakwunyere n'ime custom builds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(atọpụtaghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Directory migration ndị ọzọ dị ka ndenye `namespace=dir` nke platform path delimiter kewapụrụ (dịka `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị a hụrụ ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, ka distribution na-ebunye migrations nke ya ghara ịdaba n'otu upstream numeric slots. Ndenye na-ezighi ezi, namespace na-adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ directory na-adịghị adị ga-ebute error na startup kama ịmafe schema ahụ n'enweghị ọkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                              | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka ọtụtụ migrations na-echere (#3416). Startup ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na migrations na-echere na DB dị ugbu a karịrị ọnụọgụ a (ọ na-echebe megide tracking table e hichapụrụ). Welie ya iji weghachite backup ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_     | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Directory ọrụ maka release gate `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (install trees abụọ nke ọ bụla dị ihe dịka 3 GB tinyere tarball), ya mere, a gaghị agba ya na tmpfs pere mpe — na self-hosted runner ahụ, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado, gate ahụ mekwara ka ohere ya gwụ, nke butere mbelata package ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na code)_            | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Oge etiti flush (ms) maka batched spend/cost writer. Uru ndị dị ala na-ebelata ijikọta writes; uru ndị dị elu na-ebelata asọmpi DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na code)_            | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke spend entries a ga-edobe na buffer tupu a manye flush. Welie ya na deployments nwere QPS dị elu; wedata ya mgbe oke memory dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(atọpụtaghị)_                    | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta debug logs `[ProxyFetch]` n'ụzọ relay Vercel. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji zere ikpughe ntụziaka routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(atọpụtaghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Absolute path nke local proxy core binary (faịlụ aha ya bụ `sing-box`) nke na-enyocha core configuration emepụtara tupu ọ dochie faịlụ a nabatara. Mgbe a tọpụtaghị ya, ọ na-ede naanị `<path>.generated` n'akụkụ faịlụ a nabatara. Ọ bụ naanị maka host environment: anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ nabata ya site na management API, n'ihi na a na-agba binary ahụ ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(atọpụtaghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Otu local proxy core si reload mgbe e dochichara config a kwadoro: JSON argument array a na-agba n'enweghị shell, ma ọ bụ kpọmkwem `external` mgbe onye nlekọta dị n'èzí na-etinye faịlụ ahụ n'ọrụ. Anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ kpughee ya site na API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                           | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji tinye IP ndị ahịa/ọpụpụ na nganiihu akaụntụ n'ahịrị ndekọ usoro `[ProxyEgress]` zuru ezu. A na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ OFF na ndabara ka ndekọ usoro ghara ikpughe IP ma ọ bụ nganiihu akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(edobeghị)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta oge arịrịọ ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu oke CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaghị aka)_                   | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ụzọ doro anya nke nyocha ahụike konteena na-enyocha. Mgbe edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-enweta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtọ ya na-eme ka e jiri njedebe nlekota miri emi ahụ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(edobeghị)_                      | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị efu iji wepụta nchọpụta `[omniroute completion]` site n'ụzọ nchekwa nwa oge nke mmecha-shell CLI (ịgụ/imegharị/ide). Ọ na-abụ OFF na ndabara — nchekwa nwa oge ndị ahụ na-ada n'enweghị ozi ka nchekwa na-efu ma ọ bụ mebiri emebi ghara imebi mmecha site na taabụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (awa 24)               | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge kacha ogologo maka ịnwagharị ihe ọ bụla dị n'ìgwè (ms). A na-akara ihe ndị gafere oge a dịka ndị dara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                            | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Nkwụsị ntọala (ms) maka mmụba oge nchere n'usoro eksponenshial mgbe a na-anwagharị ihe dị n'ìgwè.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (otu awa)               | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka mmụba oge nchere n'usoro eksponenshial n'etiti ịnwagharị ihe dị n'ìgwè.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                               | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ ìgwè kacha elu a na-ahazi n'otu oge. Bulie ya iji mụbaa ọsọ nhazi; debe ya ala iji zere oke arịrịọ mberede.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe ebe nchekwa data na-adịgide adịgide dị ugbu a, ngwa mbufe na-ebipụta nnomi zuru ezu,
> nke adreesị ya dabere na ọdịnaya n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ sistemụ faịlụ
> na-akwado hard link nke dị n'otu sistemụ faịlụ, nke na-adịghị edegharị faịlụ dị adị, tinyere mmekọrịta faịlụ na-adịgide adịgide. Ndị ọbịa POSIX chọkwara
> mmekọrịta ndekọ; na Windows, Node nwere ike ịjụ aka njikwa ndekọ, ya mere OmniRoute na-echekwa mgbanwe faịlụ
> e bipụtara ma na-ewere mmekọrịta ndenye ndekọ dịka mbọ kacha mma.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` etinyere enweghị ike inye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite ga-akwụsị n'ụzọ nchekwa tupu e tinye
> mbufe. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na volium nwere ikike ndị ahụ; ejila
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa mbufe.

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                            | Nhazi                                                                          |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Mmepe mpaghara**               | Hapụ ndabara niile. Ebe nchekwa data dị na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.        |
| **Docker**                       | `DATA_DIR=/data` + tinye volium na `/data`.                                    |
| **E zoro ezo mgbe echekwara ya** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + chekwaa nnomi igodo ahụ! Ịtụfu ya = ịtụfu data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                    | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — nke nwa oge, izochi ya adịghị mkpa.           |

---

## 3. Netwọkụ & Ọdụnjikọ

| Varebụl                                     | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọdụnjikọ bụ isi maka ma Dashboard UI ma ebe njedebe API (ọnọdụ otu ọdụnjikọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(efu = mgbọrọgwụ)_                        | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ụzọ nta URL maka ije ozi OmniRoute n'azụ reverse proxy (na-ahazi Next.js `basePath`; ntụgharị auth na-eburu basePath n'uche). Dịka ọmụmaatụ, `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-etinye uru ahụ mgbe a na-eme `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); image mgbọrọgwụ ewurula tupu oge eruo nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container malitere tupu Next.js ebido. Hazie `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ origin ọha nke gụnyere otu ụzọ nta ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(efu = mgbọrọgwụ)_                        | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Oyiri `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke browser nwere ike ịhụ, nke a na-etinye ozugbo n'oge build ka ngosipụta ebe njedebe dashboard gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ na-eji `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` ma ọ bụrụ na ahazighị ya. Wughachi ya mgbe ị gbanwere ya (Next `basePath` bụ nke oge build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ahazighị = enweghị ike itinye na frame)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Nhọrọ nnabata maka itinye ibe HTML n'ime iframe. Mgbe ahazighị ya, route ọ bụla na-abịa na `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Hazie ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji jee ozi ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, login, docs, landing) na `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` ma na-enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, ka VS Code Simple Browser nwee ike igosi ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Akụkụ API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, alias ndị dị n'ọkwa mgbọrọgwụ) na-edobe header ndị siri ike n'ụzọ ọ bụla. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị enyere ya aka. Oge build: wughachi mgbe ị gbanwere ya (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka image; ịhazi ya na nrụnye ewurula tupu oge eruo enweghị mmetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-eje ozi proxy API `/v1/*` na ọdụnjikọ a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị bind maka ọdụnjikọ API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-eje ozi Dashboard UI na ọdụnjikọ a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                          | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-amachi njikọ nkata ndị na-aga n'otu oge; arịrịọ gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` yana `Retry-After`. Integer dị mma na-enyere nchedo ahụ aka; ma ọ bụrụ na ahazighị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụ `0`, ọ na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike ma pụọ iche maka gateway a mgbe a na-ejikọta instance OmniRoute n'usoro. Ọ na-enyere nyocha peer-loop mbata aka. Mkpụrụedemede anabatara: mkpụrụedemede, dijit, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; mkpụrụedemede kachasị bụ 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ntọala OmniRoute ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URL upstream ndị enyere ikike kpọmkwem na-enweta metadata peer; a naghị emetụ ndị na-eweta ọrụ ndị ọzọ niile aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke instance OmniRoute ndị a gara na mbụ nke a na-anabata na arịrịọ e jikọtara n'usoro (`1`-`32`). Instance ndị a na-emegharị ugboro ugboro ma ọ bụ oke ejirila gwụ na-eweghachi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port a na-ebipụta n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard na ọnọdụ mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port a na-ebipụta n'akụkụ host maka API na ọnọdụ mmepụta Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọ na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe a na-agba ọsọ n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ wrapper ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                    | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka sava nlekota ozugbo nke WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị bind maka sava WebSocket ozugbo. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` iji kpughee ya na LAN (haziekwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Njikọ na-eji cookie session nke dashboard ma ọ bụ igodo Bearer API nwere ikike nchịkwa (`manage`/`admin`) eme nkwenye njirimara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Origin ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ikike imepe WebSocket ozugbo. Enyerela origin dashboard loopback ikike na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke enyere ikike maka origin WebSocket ozugbo. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL origin zuru ezu), ọ na-atụnyere naanị akụkụ host — nke bara uru maka nhazi LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket dashboard ozugbo (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel nọ n'ihu sava WS (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị ga-ejikọ ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dịka ụzọ upgrade WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                     | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket ozugbo (agbanyere ya na ndabara, ma kee ya na loopback). Mgbanwe CI/harness nke na-agbanyụ skript enyemaka WebSocket ozugbo nke na-arụ ọrụ naanị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                       | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọsọ relay kwa (token, IP), arịrịọ kwa nkeji. Ọ dị na ebe nchekwa, kwa instance. `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị mma na-agbanyụ mgbochi akụkụ IP (oke DB kwa token ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
