## 7. URL & Mmekọrịta Cloud

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara                                                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                                    | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta dị n'ime ga-eji kpọọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dịka URL loopback/container ọbụlagodi mgbe e ji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dị n'ihu ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(efu)_                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL endpoint relay cloud (njirimara premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge HTTP ga-akwụsị ichere arịrịọ mmekọrịta cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                                      | Nhazi build Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge-build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modulu nwere ikike pụrụ iche n'ụzọ anụ ahụ na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _mmepụta standalone .build/_                                | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Ntọgharị oge-build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gasịrị na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(efu)_                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Nzuzo nkekọrịta a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Cloud Sync degharịa nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                                     | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ndabara ga-agbanwe gaa na mmanye na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                                     | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi na omume otu-nzọụkwụ v3.8.5 nke "bubata ihe niile" na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                                    | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị emepụtara ga-eji host reverse-proxy canonical.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(efu)_                                                     | Akụkụ onye ahịa                                  | Oyiri `CLOUD_URL` dị n'akụkụ onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Ndaghachi ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                       | _(edobeghị ya — ọ ga-eji `PORT` ma ọ bụrụ na nke a adịghị)_ | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Port ndaghachi nke akụkụ klayịentị maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin (SSR/ule); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | Onye na-achọpụta public-origin, URL onyonyo      | Origin OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-eji, nke nwere mkpa kachasị elu maka imepụta URL ọhaneze na nkwado browser-origin na-abụghị nke dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-erute OmniRoute site na URL ime, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ebudata mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọhaneze. Etinyekwala `/v1`.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru oke nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye klayịentị sidecar. Mgbe edobeghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                                      | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protokol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT n'enweghị origin arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọhaneze na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edobeghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka nkụnyeisi public-origin ezipụgoro n'ihu. Edobeghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ezipụgoro n'ihu obi naanị ma ọ bụrụ na o si na proxy loopback nwere akara token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa peer proxy private-LAN obi, ma na-enye bridge `API_PORT` ohere idowe `X-Forwarded-For` nke peer dị otú ahụ. N'ime production, họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` akọwapụtara kpọmkwem. |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọhaneze maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Override nwere mkpa kachasị elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. Ọ na-abụ ndaghachi mgbe edobeghị variable bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(edobeghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Origin ọhaneze a na-eji emepụta URL callback async. Ndaghachi nwere mkpa kacha nta maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dịka URL ọhaneze izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                                | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint nchọpụta quota CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`          | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits`   | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`           | Endpoint quota ClinePass nke awa 5 / kwa izu / kwa ọnwa nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                       | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API-key na-akwado, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta nkụnyeisi njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) na arịrịọ upstream opencode-go/zen nke klayịentị ezipụghị, ka Cloudflare na VPS egress nwee ike ịnabata ha (#6210/#5997). Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode/1.18.31`                                          | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere ma edoghị ngbanwe `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` pụrụ iche maka onye na-eweta ọrụ ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị n’ọrụ na ndị mmebe opencode. N’arịrịọ na-enweghị igodo nke ọnụ ụzọ upstream gbochiri, a ga-eji ndabara a dochie uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` kama ịjụ arịrịọ ahụ.                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`          | `1` (agbanwuru)                                             | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Ziga nkụnyeisi njirimara ngwa ahịa (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) n’ihu gaa na ndị na-eweta upstream, ka arịrịọ malitere n’ime otu harness edepụtara wee jigide njirimara ya mgbe ọ na-agafe router (ọnụ ụzọ onye mbipụta OpenRouter na-adabere na ndị a). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-edobe njirimara ndekọ/onye nchịkwa ma na-ewepụ ụkpụrụ onye ahịa nyere. Nkụnyeisi omenala onye nchịkwa haziri na-emeri ụkpụrụ onye ahịa mgbe niile.                                           |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                                   | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnyeisi `x-opencode-client` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnyeisi `x-opencode-project` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` gbanyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`     | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Otu ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị irè nke a na-ekewa ọdịda upstream ha dịka IP ọpụpụ (otu 429 na-adọba njikọ nwanne niile na-eji otu IP ahụ). Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-eji ndabara ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ usoro ọdịda a kpamkpam; ndepụta e ji kọma kewaa na-anọchi otu ahụ. A na-ekewa atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ dịka akaụntụ na upstream, ya mere enwere ike ịgbanyụ ya n’enweghị nsogbu (#9611).                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`    | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imezigharị ahụ arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo (akara streaming na ndepụta ngwaọrụ). A ka na-etinye nkụnyeisi. A na-agụ ya n’arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`   | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha ngwaọrụ e ji kọma kewaa nke a ga-ekwupụta n’arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo nke na-enweghị nke ọ bụla, mgbe ahụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, a na-eji otu placeholder nke a gwara model ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndenye na-ezighi ezi anya.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                               | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud eji ewepụta ozi oke ojiji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / ngwa nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`     | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                            | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Endpoint upstream ego fọdụrụ nke Ollama Cloud dere n’akwụkwọ (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe module na-eburu. A na-ebu ụzọ jiri ya maka oke ojiji Ollama Cloud mgbe njikọ nwere API key; ihe na-ewepụta ozi site na cookie ibe ntọala ka na-abụ nhọrọ ndabere. Gbanwee ya maka relays / ngwa nnwale.                                                                                                                    |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` eji ewepụta ozi oke ojiji site na ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var ọzọ maka cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(edoghị)_                                                  | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var ọzọ maka cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` nke a na-eji tupu aliases ndị dị mkpụmkpụ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ebe njedebe oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebuga ya n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike nke gị (dịka, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha emepụtara kwesịrị iji hostname ahụ. Enweghị nke a, OAuth callbacks nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adakọ, njikọ ọha emepụtara nwekwara ike ịtụ aka na origin dị n'ime container.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dịka URL loopback/container dị n'ime maka ọrụ server-to-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ hostname ọha maka internal self-fetches ndị bu credential.
>
> Ndekọ dashboard ndị enyochara njirimara anaghị achọ static public base URL: dashboard ahụ na-eziga same-origin unsafe requests site na CSRF token ejikọtara na session. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta public-origin validation n'otu ebe maka non-dashboard browser integrations: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị explicit public URL env vars obi; a na-eleghara raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` headers anya ma e wezụga ma agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma tinyekwa token n'ahụ immediate proxy peer iji gosi na a tụkwasịrị ya obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie same-origin dashboard requests; CORS bụ naanị maka cross-origin browser clients.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Mee ka oku ndị na-aga na ndị na-eweta LLM dị n'elu gafee na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka ịchịkwa ọpụpụ, iduzi dịka mpaghara ala, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` na-eji `true` dịka ndabara. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ
`off` iji manye HTTP/1.1 na ndị Undici dispatcher dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: ọpụpụ ozugbo, proxy HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relay, na
nnwale ọhụrụ ha na socket ọhụrụ. Nke a na-echekwa nyocha asambodo TLS, njirimara
proxy, oke njikọ, na ntọala keep-alive. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe
ị gbanwechara ya n'ihi na a na-edobe ndị dispatcher na cache, a na-emepụtakwa ndị relay agent mgbe mmalite.
Mgbanwe a anaghị achịkwa wreq TLS fingerprinting ma ọ bụ usoro mbufe akọwapụtara maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`     | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mee ka onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 rụọ ọrụ maka oku upstream. Jiri `false` họrọ ịghara iji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`     | Akụkụ klayenti                                 | Nghọta dị n'akụkụ klayenti banyere nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara a na-agbanye na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nọchiri anya dị na DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ). Ọdọ proxy na ntụgharị nke akaụntụ ọ bụla na-akwụsị inyeghachi otu onye otu dara ugbu a (nnwale TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 e nwetara site na ya) ruo oge nke na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọdịda ahụ mere ọzọ, ruo n'ókè kachasị. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                                       |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(edoghị)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta ikike maka ebe njikwa-selector nke local-core gafere loopback (aha ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IP ndị e ji rikoma/ohere kewaa; `*` na-emepe LAN/nkeonwe mana ọ naghị emepe metadata/link-local ma ọlị). A na-ekwe loopback site na http/https na ndabara. Uru DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ karịa mgbanwe a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị oghere.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`   | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ, nke na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọ mere ọzọ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`   | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Ókè kachasị (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe ókè kachasị dị ala karịa ntọala, ọ ga-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata ókè kachasị na-emekwa ka oge ichefu usoro ahụ dị mkpụmkpụ: a na-ehichapụ ndenye `2 x max` mgbe ha kubiri ume.                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`    | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nọchiri anya dị na DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ). Maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke a na-ekewa oke ha dịka adreesị ọpụpụ si dị, debe onye otu ọdọ nke na-eji otu adreesị ọpụpụ ahụrụ anya dị ka onye otu a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ozugbo n'okpuru ndị otu ahụ siri ike. Ọ bụ naanị nhazi n'usoro; anaghị ewepụ ya ma ọlị. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbaàmà ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`   | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Windo nlọghachi (ms) nke na-amachi mkpu webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye otu ọdọ ọ bụla ka ọtụtụ ọjụjụ n'otu ntabi anya zipụ naanị otu mmemme. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ bụla a na-ezipụ ya. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara n'enweghị ọkwa.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTP maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTPS maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edoghị)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-ekedo ebe echo-IP nke nyocha ọpụpụ proxy na-eji n'otu URL. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-ebu ụzọ nwalee `api64.ipify.org`, mesịa `api4.ipify.org`, ka a ghara ịkọwa tunnel ndị na-akwado naanị IPv4 dị ka ndị nwụrụ anwụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Aha host/IP ndị e ji rikoma kewaa, nke a ga-agafe proxy maka ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`       | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ socket kachasị elu nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge maka dispatcher proxy HTTP/SOCKS ọ bụla echekwara na cache. Stream SSE ndị na-adịte aka, dịka Codex `/v1/responses`, chọrọ ihe karịrị otu njikọ mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy dị n'ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-amachi ụkpụrụ karịrị `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`    | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge nchere kachasị maka handshake (njikọ) SOCKS5 na ms. Bulie ya mgbe nnukwu ọrụ n'otu oge rutere otu host gateway residential (dịka arịrịọ 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafe 10s mgbe pool jupụtara, ọbụlagodi na enwere ike iru proxy ahụ; ma ọ bụghị ya, nke a ga-apụta n'ụzọ na-ezighi ezi dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. A na-amachi ya na `120000`.                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`    | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a na-**ajụ (fail-closed)** arịrịọ nke proxy e kenyere ya enweghị ike idozi, kama ịlaghachi na njikọ ozugbo — nke a na-egbochi mkpughe real-IP. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachi fallback DIRECT ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`    | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js mee ka fingerprint TLS yie nke ọzọ (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Nke a na-egbochi mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ndị provider a na-anabata maka routing TLS ọhụrụ site na proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`), nke e ji rikoma kewaa. TLS ozugbo na-edobe omume ochie ya mgbe edoghị ya; naanị provider ndị a ka a na-ebufe site na bridge fingerprint Chrome-124. A na-ewepụ Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edoghị allowlist ahụ, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-akpalite Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici nke sistemụ n'onwe ya na-erute API ahụ.                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`    | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka context browser Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie certificate HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Ọnọdụ eji eme ihe

| Ọnọdụ                                            | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na tunnel SSH**                    | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP nke ụlọ ọrụ**                       | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                          | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (gụnyere ya)                                                              |
| **Ọpụpụ a na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo**    | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrịọ ga-ada kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị, kama ikpughe site na njikọ ozugbo.      |
| **Usoro ochie / mmepe — kwe ka fallback ozugbo** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu e mesie nchekwa ike: a na-eji njikọ ozugbo mgbe idozi proxy dara.         |

> **Rịba ama (ịgafe nkwado NVIDIA — #3226):** Endpoint NVIDIA maka nkwado API-key
> na-akwụsị mgbe e zigara ya site na fetch proxy/TLS emezigharịrị nke zuru ụwa ọnụ (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe
> patch proxy maka naanị oku nkwado ahụ site n'iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ ihe pụrụ iche edepụtara nke oke ya doro anya — ọ **dịghị** emetụta ọpụpụ chat/usage.
> `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-ekedo oke nke bypass ahụ.

---

## 9. Njikọta Ngwa CLI

Na-achịkwa otu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Ihe mgbanwee                      | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ n'ime PATH nke sistemụ; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ ndị akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ntinye PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọta faịlụ binari CLI (e ji kọlọn kewaa ha).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ home a na-eji agụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị ụzọ zuru ezu ma dị n'ime home nke process ahụ — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e ji bind-mount (ọ bụ otu `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-alaghachi na ndekọ home. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (imegharị token, data session). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka mbọ ọ bụla ide nhazi CLI daa site na njehie doro anya "anaghị ekwe ka e dee ihe".                                                                        |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binari Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binari Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binari Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binari OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, emesịa `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binari nke Cursor agent. ### Mmekọrịta Akpaghị Aka nke Profailụ CLI

A naghị agbanye feature flag ndị a na ndabara; a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye ha. A pụkwara ịgbanwuo ha site na
dashboard CLI Code.

| Agbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model provider gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profailụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Codex nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model provider gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profailụ Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Claude nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binary ndị dị na host n'ime container ma gwa OmniRoute ebe ha dị:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e mere ka ụzọ ahụ bụrụ bind mount site na
host — jikọta ya na mount dịka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ
`host` dị na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ na-adịghị n'ime home nke
onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ nke na-abụghị bind mount anya, n'ihi na a ga-ehichapụ
ihe e dere ebe ahụ mgbe e megharịrị container ahụ.

Image ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dịka `USER node`, ya mere `/root` a na-emeghị mount **abụghị**
override ziri ezi.

| Variable                                 | Nke ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaka)_  | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Manye nchọpụta container ka ọ gbanye (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). A chọrọ ya naanị na runtime nke nchọpụta akpaka na-agaghị achọpụta.                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka ngwá ọrụ CLI dee config n'ụzọ container a na-emeghị mount n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ. Ihe CLI ya na ya hà bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                     |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | edoghị ya   | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Naanị Docker image: **Update Now** dị na dashboard na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (ọ ga-adị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) ka watcher dị n'akụkụ host nwee ike ịdọta ma megharịa container ahụ. Lee [Nduzi Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Ihe enyemaka CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Variable ndị a na-ahazi omume nke CLI binary `omniroute` n'onwe ya (ọ bụghị
nchọpụta sidecar dị n'elu).

| Variable                              | Nke ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemu)_                       | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Manye asụsụ mmepụta CLI. Locale BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-akacha variable locale nke sistemu (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ stdout/stderr nke server na terminal n'ụdị supervised. Ọ hà na flag `--log` dị na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth a na-etinye dịka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka na task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Root home Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; run otu ugboro na-eji home nwa oge nke e kewapụrụ iche.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge amara na millisecond tupu e jiri ike kwụsị CLI child e zigara signal.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ 1 yana DATA_DIR akọwapụtara iji mafere faịlụ environment nke data-directory onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token sync maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị token sync, ọ bụghị API key). Ọ hà na `--token`.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Timeout HTTP kwa nnwale (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-echere endpoint ahụike server tupu o bipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị timeout. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi ndị na-egbu oge (dịka Windows). A pụkwara ịtọ ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory ahaziri iche maka nchọpụta plugin CLI (package `omniroute-cmd-*`). Mgbe edoghị ya, ndabara bụ `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Naanị CLI — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-erute scanner plugin dị n'akụkụ server, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-arụtụ aka na ya (ngalaba 2).                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(edoghị ya)_                     | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Gbochie ọkwa update CLI mgbe process na-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na atọrọ ya na uru ọ bụla na-adịghị efu. A ka na-agbanyụ ọkwa ahụ n'onwe ya na CI, quiet mode, na ụdị mmepụta na-abụghị ederede.                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(e sitere na ya nweta)_          | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | URL liveness nke `omniroute doctor` na-enyocha. Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` nke program na-ebute ụzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji uru a kpọmkwem dịka ọ dị. Mgbe edoghị ya, CLI na-ewepụta endpoint `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ catalog provider nke iwu provider CLI na-eji. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` nke program na-ebute ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ absolute dịka ha dị; ụzọ relative na-amalite site na root project. Mgbe edoghị ya, CLI na-eji directory constants provider, ebe faịlụ ochie `providers.ts` bụ fallback. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara provider)_              | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Model fallback zuru ụwa ọnụ maka nnwale njikọ provider CLI. Usoro ibute ụzọ bụ model njikọ ahaziri, override model akọwapụtara maka provider, uru a, wee bụrụ model nnwale arụnyere n'ime provider.                                                                                  |
