## 10. Njikọ Agent Dị N'ime & MCP

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                             | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka                                | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL akọwapụtara nke ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji ruo OmniRoute. Ọ na-akagbu nchọpụta localhost akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(edoghị)_                                          | Modulu MCP/A2A                                              | Igodo API maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP dị n'ime na oku nka A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edoghị)_                                          | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID igodo maka ikenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edoghị)_                                          | Nke ochie                                                   | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a na-edobe akụkọ ihe aga-eme A2A na nchekwa data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ na ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ ga-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Mgbanwe nkwụsị ozugbo maka atụmatụ nlele memory-hits nke A2A. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji mafere kpamkpam nyocha iweghachite ebe nchekwa maka ihe aga-eme; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edoghị ya) ga-ahapụ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka endpoint nhazi recorded-triage nke Issue Agent na-enweghị ịntanetị/mpaghara rụọ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ ma e wezụga mgbe a na-eme usoro ọrụ recorded-triage mpaghara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(edoghị)_                                          | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge nchere kacha (ms) maka otu ọsọ recorded-triage nke Issue Agent. A na-amachi ya n'ogo kacha elu dị n'ime; ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara arụnyere mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ na-ezighị ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ)_                                  | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Ọnọdụ/profaịlụ remote-mode nke CLI maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-akagbu ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ n'ebe nchekwa ọnọdụ mpaghara. Ya na `--context <name>` bụ otu ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend OS-keychain `keytar` nke nhọrọ maka nzere ọnọdụ CLI. Mgbe agbanyere ya, nzere ga-anọgide na ọnọdụ `config.json` `0600`, CLI ga-ewepụtakwa ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro; e mere ya maka ọrụ headless/container a kpachapụrụ anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa nnweta dabere na scope n'oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scope ndị rikoma kewara: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Kpakọba nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu ịgbanwe manifest ka ọ bụrụ usoro e nwere ike izipu. Uru ndị na-eme ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algọridim/profaịlụ mkpakọ. Uru ndị na-eme ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ọgụgụ njikwa dị n'ime MCP-server (ahụike, nkwụsi ike, combos, quota, ojiji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke mmebi (ms) maka njikọ MCP ndị na-echere onye na-eweta (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke ihe ngosi ndeksi local-corpus echekwara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu maka akwụkwọ ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ ọ bụla e tinyere na ndeksi). A na-amanye ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model, n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Ogologo oge n'etiti nyocha oke ọnụego na oke ojiji nke onye na-eweta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere oge (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke OAuth ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ime nnukwu mmekọrịta; a na-enweta njikọ OAuth otu n'otu iji zere izigara usoro dị n'elu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu ntabi anya. `0` na-ewepụ atụmatụ a (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oge kacha nta (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke ndị na-esochi ibe ha site n'usoro dị n'elu n'ụzọ preflight/monitor nke arịrịọ ọ bụla; ọ na-ekesa oku netwọk ndị na-eme n'otu oge ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ dị n'otu IP ghara izigara usoro dị n'elu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu ntabi anya. Ejikọtara ya na ndị na-eweta nnweta oke nke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (ebe nnweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izugbe `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` maka iziga arịrịọ (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) abanyeghị na nke a — a na-enyocha ya iche. Ọ naghị emetụta nnweta sitere na cache. `0` na-gbanyụọ ya; a na-amanye ya ka ọ dị n'etiti `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oghere oge enweghị usoro ọzọ maka arịrịọ ọ bụla (ms), nke a na-ahọpụta n'oge nnweta oke ọ bụla edebere site n'usoro dị n'elu. Ndabara bụ 120; a na-amanye ya ka ọ dị n'etiti `0..60000`; `0` na-ewepụ naanị jitter. Tọọ `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` iji gbanyụọ njikwa ọsọ ahụ dum. Jitter anaghị ekwe nkwa igbochi mmachibido iwu sitere n'usoro dị n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge nchere (ms) tupu imegharị oke onye na-eweta mgbe ezigbo ihe omume ojiji gasịrị, iji nye API oke nke usoro dị n'elu oge iji dekọọ ojiji ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Ụzọ zuru ezu gaa na faịlụ Chrome ma ọ bụ Edge nke sistemụ nwere ike ime, nke a na-eji maka nbanye Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta na mmegharị na-anọghị n'ihu ihuenyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | agbanyere                                           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ site na nnọkọ Chrome CDP nke akaụntụ ọ bụla. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ mmegharị ihe nchọgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | agbanyere                                           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-edobe nnọkọ Adobe ndị arụziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` mgbe usoro malitegharịrị. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji debe nnọkọ naanị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oghere oge kacha nta n'ime millisekọnd n'etiti nnyefe mmepụta Adobe Firefly; `0` na-gbanyụọ oghere oge ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oge jụụ ọzọ n'ime millisekọnd mgbe nnyefe Adobe ọ bụla nke atọ gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Oge ndabere maka ichere n'ime millisekọnd mgbe nzaghachi Adobe 408 nwa oge gasịrị; a na-ejikọta ya na oghere oge nnyefe n'ime nnwale ndị na-agaghị agafe ise.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Gbanyụọ ọrụ ndabere niile (mmekọrịta, ịtọ ọnụahịa, imegharị model). Ọ bara uru maka CI/nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(edoghị)_                                          | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Mbido naanị maka injin proxy: ọ na-amafe subsistemụ nhọrọ, `/dashboard` → 404. Ọ bụ otu ihe ahụ dị ka `omniroute serve --headless`. Lee [HEADLESS.md](../guides/HEADLESS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(edoghị ya)_                                       | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Manyere ka ọrụ ndabere rụọ ọrụ mgbe achọpụtara ule akpaaka. | `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Ugboro a na-enyocha nrụgharị mmefu ego (ms). Opekempe `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`     | `60000`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Ugboro mgbake njikọ site na cooldown nke a na-ebute ụzọ (ms): ọ na-enyochagharị njikọ ndị `rate_limited_until` nwa oge ha gafere, n'èzí ụzọ arịrịọ a na-ejikarị eme ihe. Opekempe `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY`         | `false`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Gbanyụọ onye nhazi mgbake njikọ site na cooldown nke a na-ebute ụzọ (mgbake umengwụ n'ime `getProviderCredentials` ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_CACHE_CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000`                                           | `src/lib/jobs/reasoningCacheCleanupJob.ts`                  | Ugboro nhicha cache echiche (ms). Opekempe `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_EFFORT_PROBE_PROVIDERS`    | _edoghị ya_ (agbanyụrụ)                             | `open-sse/services/learnedReasoningEffortCaps.ts`           | Nhọrọ-mbanye, NJ ndị na-eweta ọrụ e ji rikoma kewaa (ma ọ bụ `*`): mgbe njehie `reasoning_effort` 400/422 na-akọwaghị otu ụkpụrụ anabatara, nwaa ọzọ otu ọkwa ala (agaghị adị n'okpuru `low`) ma mụta ọkwa ahụ naanị ma ọ bụrụ na enwetara nzaghachi nye nyocha ahụ (#14895).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CRON`                     | `0 * * * *`                                         | `src/lib/jobs/logExportJob.ts`                              | Nkwupụta Cron (UTC) maka ọrụ mbupụ ndekọ oku nke na-ewepụ data n'ebe mbupụ ndekọ niile agbanyere. | `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_RUN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `900000`                                            | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Oge ngwụcha elekere mgbidi nke usoro nwa ọ bụla maka ebe mbupụ, na milisekọnd. Ngwụcha oge na-edekọ ọdịda ma kwụsị usoro ahụ n'ebughị ogbe a na-akwadobeghị n'ihu. Ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi/na-abụghị nke dị mma/na-abụghị nọmba zuru oke na-eji ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CHILD_MAX_OLD_SPACE_MB`   | `1024`                                              | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Oke old-space V8 nke usoro nwa mbupụ Node na MB (ọ bụghị RSS niile). Ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi/na-abụghị nke dị mma/na-abụghị nọmba zuru oke na-eji ndabara. Mbupụ Bun na-alaghachi n'ịrụ ọrụ n'ime usoro ahụ na-enweghị oke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONFIG_HOT_RELOAD_MS`                | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/config/hotReload.ts`                               | Etiti oge nyocha (ms) maka nbugharị-ọkụ nhazi. A na-ajụ nke dị ala karịa `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_REDIS_AUTH_CACHE`            | _(agbanyere)_                                       | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                     | Tọọ `1` iji gafere cache nkwenye API-key nke Redis na-akwado (na-amanye ịgụ DB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS`           | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/rtk/filterLoader.ts` | Tụkwasị iwu nzacha ọrụ RTK ndị ọrụ na-ejikwa obi na-enweghị nyocha mbinye aka siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_LITE_MAX_TOOL_LENGTH`                | `2000`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/lite.ts`                     | Oke mkpụrụedemede maka mkpirisi nsonaazụ-ngwaọrụ Lite a na-ebute ụzọ mgbe edoghị `lite.maxToolLength`. Oke 256–1000000. Ntọala dashboard na-emeri env a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKERS`                      | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke ndị ọrụ RTK/Caveman mmekọrịta n'otu oge; ọrụ ndị karịrị ya na-echere n'usoro FIFO.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                            | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oge ngwụcha ọrụ ọ bụla na milisekọnd. A na-akwụsị ndị ọrụ oge ha gwụrụ, arịrịọ ahụ adaakwa n'enweghị mgbanwee.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ogologo oge ịnọ nkịtị na milisekọnd tupu akwụsị onye ọrụ mkpakọ a na-ejighị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_MAX_OLD_MB`            | `1024`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oke heap nke old-generation V8 na MB maka onye ọrụ mkpakọ ọ bụla, n'ụdị niile (ala kacha nta `128`). Ọrụ gafere ya na-emepe ma daa (na-enweghị mkpakọ) kama ime ka heap nke gateway bawanye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Mgbanwe ukwu nke circuit-breaker maka engine ọ bụla n'ime stacked-pipeline T02. **A ga-ahọrọ iji ya (agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara)** — mgbe agbanyere ya, a na-amafe engine nke na-atụpụ njehie ugboro ugboro n'ofe arịrịọ (fail-open) ruo oge cooldown; mgbe agbanyụrụ ya = omume legacy yiri kpọmkwem n'ogo byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda na-aga n'ihu n'ofe arịrịọ tupu breaker nke engine emeghee.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Millisekọnd ole a ga-anọgide na-amafe engine mepere emepe tupu nyocha half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ramp nzaghachi-nweghachite CCR T08/H8: nweghachite ọ bụla gara aga nke block echekwara na-ebuli `minChars` ya dị irè n'usoro kwụ ọtọ (ọdịnaya a na-eweghachite ugboro ugboro na-enweta mkpakọ pere mpe; nweghachite `>=3` = anaghị enweta mkpakọ ma ọlị). `1` na-agbanyụ ramp ahụ (naanị binary skip na threshold).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ebe nchekwa block na-adịgide adịgide nke CCR (#9061). Ọ na-eji SQLite kwado ebe nchekwa dị na memory ka block nwee ike ịlanarị nchụpụ LRU, TTL, mmalitegharị, ma ọ bụ nweghachite rutere na instance ọzọ. Tọọ `false` iji debe block naanị na memory. Block karịrị 512KB na cloud runtime ga-anọ naanị na memory n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Mgbanwe ukwu nke prefix freeze T08/H5 a hụrụ site n'ojiji. **A ga-ahọrọ iji ya (agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara)** — mgbe agbanyere ya, a na-ewere system prompt a hụrụ `>=` threshold dị ka prefix kwụsiri ike nke a pụrụ itinye na cache, ma chekwaa ya ka mkpakọ ghara imetụta ya ọbụna maka provider ndị static cache heuristic na-agaghị ahụ (freeze naanị _na-echekwa_, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ oge a ga-ahụ system prompt tupu ewere ya dị ka prefix kwụsiri ike e freeze-ere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Bootstrap script na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` mgbe nhazi mbụ gachara. Ọ na-achịkwa ma setup wizard ga-apụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Ụzọ mgbapụ: kwe ka body arịrịọ dochie project field nke Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Iwu Google One AI credits: `off` anaghị etinye credits ma ọlị, `retry` na-etinye ha otu ugboro mgbe quota 429 tozuru etozu gasịrị, ebe `always` na-etinye ha n'arịrịọ mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Kwe ka onye ntụgharị arịrịọ Antigravity mafere nyocha siri ike nke signature arịrịọ CLI mgbe upstream jụrụ signature ndị bụ eziokwu (ọnọdụ debug/CLI mgbe ochie). Uru na-abụghị efu na-eme ka bypass ahụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Dochie ụzọ token-file nke Antigravity CLI (agy) maka mbubata local login a na-achọpụta na-akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (N'ime Sistemụ)

| Variable            | Ndabara              | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite               | Nkọwa                                        |
| ------------------- | -------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL server maka njikọ nkwenye CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nkwenye maka njikọ CLI.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`                | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID onye ọrụ maka session njikọ CLI.          |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ legacy maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.      |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ legacy maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.       |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(edobeghị)_         | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ọzọ legacy maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.     |
