## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Nchịkwa siri ike maka mmekọrịta ọnụahịa models.dev. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya = soro Ntọala > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **na-emeri DB** ma na-amafere ma mmekọrịta oge niile ma nyocha SQL/JSON nke `getModelsDevPricing()` (mgbake mgbe dashboard rapaara n'otu event loop ahụ). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` na-amanye ka mmekọrịta rụọ ọrụ. Nchekwa/nhichapụ ọnụahịa ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke anaghị eme ihe n'okpuru mmachi nke nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'oge mmepe, na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (na sekọnd) maka ihe mmezi context-window nke na-emezi onwe ya (5004): ọ na-edobe windo ndị provider kwupụtara site na nchọpụta `/models` dịka mgbanwe `auto:discovery` mgbe ha dị iche na katalọgụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. Ọ na-eji data emekọrịtalarị ọzọ (enweghị fetch ọhụrụ); ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-edegharị mgbanwe `manual`.                                                                                                                                                            |

---

## 20. Ntọala Ndị Kpọmkwem Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ

| Ngbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OPENROUTER_CATALOG_TTL_MS`                    | `86400000` (24h)                         | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterCatalog.ts`                                             | TTL nke cache katalọgụ ụdị OpenRouter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MODEL_CATALOG_INCLUDE_NAMES`                  | `true`                                   | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                   | Tinye oghere `name` ndị dị mfe igosipụta na nzaghachi `/v1/models`. Gbanyụọ ya maka ndị ahịa na-atụ anya naanị ID.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CATALOG_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `8000` (8s)                              | `src/app/api/v1/models/catalogCache.ts`                                            | Oke oge nchere n'ụzọ oyi maka nrụgharị katalọgụ `GET /v1/models` e jikọtara ọnụ (#12627). Mgbe oge gwụrụ, a na-enye nzaghachi 200 ikpeazụ dị mma ma ọ bụrụ na ọ dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNCED_CATALOG_STALE_AFTER_MS`      | `2592000000` (ụbọchị 30)                 | `src/lib/db/models/activeSyncedCatalog.ts`                                         | Afọ nke, mgbe ọ gafesịrị, ndepụta ụdị emekọrịtara nke njikọ agaghịzi abụ isi ikike maka ntụgharị ụzọ, kama ọ ga-ahapụ arịrịọ ka ọ gaa na ndekọ (#12849). Ahịrị ndị a na-enyetụbeghị timestamp na-agụ dịka ndị kara nká.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60s)                            | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Ogologo oge a ga-eji ọzọ nzaghachi `GET /api/usage/analytics` dị nwayọ maka otu query string ahụ (#15629). `0` na-agbanyụọ cache ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`     | `1000` (1s)                              | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Naanị nzaghachi nyocha data nke were opekata mpe oge a iji wuo ka a na-etinye na cache, ka instance ndị nta na-ahụ data ọhụrụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CACHE_METRICS_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`       | edoghị (agbanyụrụ)                       | `src/app/api/cache/route.ts` site na `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`     | Nhọrọ ịbanye (#16361). Mgbe atọrọ ya ka ọ bụrụ integer N na-adịghị n'okpuru efu, a na-enye akụkụ akụkọ ojiji nke `GET /api/cache` (ngụkọta prompt-cache + usoro mgbanwe) site na cache nzaghachi nyocha data e kesara ma ọ bụrụ na o were opekata mpe N ms iji wuo, maka windo `trendHours` ọ bụla e tinyere oke, ruo `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (akụkọ nwere ike ịla azụ ruo TTL ahụ; `DELETE /api/cache` na-eme ka ọ gharazie ịdị irè). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ naghị arụ ọrụ = a na-agụ akụkọ ozugbo na arịrịọ ọ bụla. |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `120000`                                 | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Oge nchere kachasị maka ọrụ mmepụta onyonyo NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `2500`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Ugboro ole a na-enyocha ọnọdụ ọrụ NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`           | `8000`                                   | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyUpscale.ts`                                         | Nkwụsị oge ntọala maka exponential backoff nke ịnwa ọzọ iziga ọrụ mmụba ogo Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AWS_REGION`                                   | _(edoghị)_                               | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Mpaghara eji emepụta endpoint AWS Bedrock (Kiro, ọdịyo).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`                           | _(edoghị)_                               | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Nhọrọ ndabere mgbe edoghị `AWS_REGION`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`                        | _(edoghị)_                               | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-ai.ts`                                              | ID akaụntụ maka Cloudflare Workers AI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLOUDFLARE_PLAYGROUND_CHROME_PATH`            | _(edoghị)_                               | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`                                      | Ụzọ zuru ezu nke binary Chrome desktọpụ maka executor Cloudflare AI Playground, nke a na-eji mgbe nyocha fingerprint headless gbochiri Chromium Playwright jikọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEBUDDY_SYSTEM_PROMPT_MAX_LEN`              | ∅ (∞) — _(edoghị = enweghị oke ogologo)_ | `open-sse/executors/codebuddy/shared.ts`                                           | Oke ogologo nhọrọ maka system prompt onye na-akpọ oku zigara CodeBuddy (CN na International). Na ndabara, naanị prompt nwere akara njirimara coding-agent ka a na-eji nke na-anọpụ iche dochie (nzacha ọdịnaya Tencent na-ajụ ha); a na-edobe prompt ndị ogologo. Integer > 0 na-edochikwa system prompt ọ bụla toro ogologo karịa ọnụọgụ mkpụrụedemede ahụ; ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụ `0`, ma ọ bụ na ọ bụghị integer, a na-agbanyụọ oke ahụ. Ndochi ọ bụla na-edekọ iwu kpatara ya (akara njirimara ma ọ bụ ogologo).                 |
| `CLOUDFLARE_API_BASE`                          | `https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4`   | `src/app/api/settings/proxy/cloudflare-deploy/route.ts`                            | Dochie ntọala Cloudflare REST API nke proxy-pool Workers relay deployer na-eji (#4640 / 9router#1360).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDFLARE_RELAY_DEFAULT_PROJECT` | `omniroute-relay`                        | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/CloudflareRelayModal.tsx` | Aha ndabara nke ọrụ worker a na-atụ aro na modal "Deploy Relay" nke proxy-pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDFLARE_RELAY_ENABLED`         | `true`                                   | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/ProxyPoolTab.tsx`         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji zoo nhọrọ relay Cloudflare Workers na taabụ Proxy Pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLOUDFLARED_BIN`                              | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka                     | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary `cloudflared`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLOUDFLARED_PROTOCOL`                         | `http2`                                  | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Protocol mbufe tunnel: `http2` (ndabara), `quic`, ma ọ bụ `auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLOUDFLARED_CONFIG`                           | _(edobeghị)_                             | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ụzọ gaa na `config.yml` cloudflared a na-achịkwa na mpaghara (nke nwere `tunnel:`, `credentials-file:`, `ingress:`). Mgbe edobere ya, OmniRoute na-agba `tunnel --config <path> run` (tunnel nwere aha) kama quick tunnel nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUDFLARED_HOSTNAME`                         | _(site na ingress nhazi)_                | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ndochi maka hostname ọha nke tunnel nwere aha (dịka `ai.example.com`), nke a na-akọ dị ka `publicUrl`/`apiUrl`. Mgbe edobeghị ya, a na-agụ ya site na hostname `ingress` mbụ dị na nhazi ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DENO_DEPLOY_API_BASE`                         | `https://api.deno.com/v2`                | `src/app/api/settings/proxy/deno-deploy/route.ts`                                  | Dochie ntọala Deno Deploy REST API nke onye na-ebuga relay proxy-pool ji eme ihe (#4643 / 9router#1437).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_DENO_RELAY_DEFAULT_PROJECT`       | `omniroute-deno-relay`                   | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/DenoRelayModal.tsx`       | Aha ndabara nke ngwa Deno Deploy a na-atụ aro na modal "Deploy Relay" nke proxy-pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_DENO_RELAY_ENABLED`               | `true`                                   | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/ProxyPoolTab.tsx`         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji zoo nhọrọ relay Deno Deploy na taabụ Proxy Pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                          | `300000` (nkeji 5)                       | `open-sse/services/searchCache.ts`                                                 | TTL maka ịchekwa nzaghachi API ọchụchọ (Perplexity, Brave, wdg.) na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER`                | `false`                                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                 | Gosi UI nnwale nke provider dakọtara na CC maka relay ndị bụ naanị Claude Code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NINEROUTER_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                              | `open-sse/executors/ninerouter.ts`                                                 | Dochie host ebe instance 9router agbakwunyere na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NINEROUTER_PORT`                              | `20130`                                  | `open-sse/executors/ninerouter.ts`                                                 | Dochie port ebe instance 9router agbakwunyere na-ege ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `EMBED_WS_PROXY_PORT`                          | `20131`                                  | `src/lib/services/embedWsProxy.ts`                                                 | Port maka sava proxy WebSocket nke ọrụ agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| `CLIPROXYAPI_PORT`                             | `5544`                                   | `open-sse/executors/cliproxyapi.ts`                                                | Port bridge CLIProxyAPI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_API_KEY`                          | _(efu)_                                  | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/cliproxyapiCredentials.ts`                             | Igodo data-plane eji dochie anya mgbe ntọala `cliproxyapi_api_key` adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_MANAGEMENT_KEY`                   | _(efu)_                                  | `src/lib/services/cliproxyAccountHealth.ts`                                        | Igodo njikwa maka ịgụ ọnọdụ akaụntụ site na instance CLIProxyAPI a na-achịkwa na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLIPROXYAPI_CONFIG_DIR`                       | `~/.cli-proxy-api`                       | `src/lib/versionManager/processManager.ts`                                         | Ndekọ nhazi CLIProxyAPI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLIPROXY_BIND_HOST`                           | `127.0.0.1`                              | `docker-compose.yml`                                                               | Host interface nke docker-compose na-ebipụta sidecar `cliproxyapi` na ya (#12578). Data volume ya na-echekwa nzere OAuth/API nke provider, ma image ahụ a kpọgidere enweghị override `api-keys` nke data-plane sitere na env (naanị `config.yaml` e-mount-ere), ya mere `0.0.0.0` na-eme ka ọrụ nwere nzere pụta ìhè nye LAN niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MUX_SERVICE_PORT`                             | `8322`                                   | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Gbanwee port ebe daemon nhazi agent nke Mux (coder/mux) agbakwunyere na-ege ntị (mgbe niile 127.0.0.1).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENWA_SERVICE_PORT`                          | `8323`                                   | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                                    | Gbanwee port ebe daemon open-wa (akpaaka WhatsApp Web) agbakwunyere na-ege ntị (mgbe niile 127.0.0.1).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                              | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Host bind/connect nke ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere (naanị loopback na ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                   | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Port nke ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                              | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Host bind/connect nke ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere (naanị loopback na ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                   | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Port nke ọrụ Dario agbakwunyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(efu)_                                  | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rịkọm kewaa, ndị a na-ewere dị ka "mpaghara" (aha ọrụ Docker, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relay ndị ọzọ na-abụghị nke mbụ, ndị na-anabata ndị ahịa Claude Code
naanị. OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrịọ ka relay ndị ahụ nwee ike ịnabata ha. Ọ bụrụ naanị na ịchọrọ iji
Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ na ị maghị ihe relay ndị a bụ, hapụ nke a ka ọ bụrụ ihe agbanyụghị ma tinye provider
nkịtị dakọtara na Anthropic kama.

---

## 21. Ahụike Proxy

| Mgbanwe                                           | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                             | Oge ngwụcha maka nyocha ahụike ọdịda-ngwa-ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                              | Oghere oge (n'ime awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxy ndị a na-atụle n'ime atụmatụ pool ahaziri maka latency.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                             | TTL nke cache maka nsonaazụ nyocha ahụike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                             | TTL nke cache maka nyocha ahụike proxy ndị dara ada. Debe nke a ka ọ dị mkpụmkpụ karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka timeout proxy na-adịru nwa oge n'okpuru concurrency dị elu nwee ike ịnwale ọzọ ngwa ngwa n'emeghị ka fast-fail kwụsị ịrụ ọrụ maka proxy ndị nwụrụ anwụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji gbanyụọ scheduler ahụike proxy nke ndabere, nke na-enyocha proxy ndị edebanyere aha site n'oge ruo n'oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Etiti oge sweep nke scheduler ahụike ndabere n'ime ms (opekata mpe `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                                | Oge etiti nyocha na ms maka proxy edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), nke nyocha ha na-akpọte serverless isolate a na-akwụ ụgwọ maka ya (opekata mpe `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Oge etiti maka usoro mgbake ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a ga-enyocha ọzọ proxy ndị na-adịghị mma na mbụ ka proxy gbakere nwee ike ịlaghachi na ntụgharị n’enweghị ịmalitegharịa. Uru ndị dị n'okpuru `60000` ga-alaghachi na uru ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                 | Ebe nyocha nnweta nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-eji. Tụnye ya aka na URL dị n'ime/onwe-gị-na-akwado iji zere uru ndabara ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                 | Ọnụọgụ nyocha a na-amalite n'otu oge n'ime batch ọ bụla, nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. A na-eme ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru 1 ma debe oke ya na 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                                 | Oge nkwụsị na ms n'etiti mpụpụ nyocha abụọ n'ime otu batch. Na-enweghị ya, batch ahụ dum na-apụ n'otu oge, IP egress a na-ekekọrịta nwekwara ike ịkpalite ebe e debere oke ọnụego. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nkewa oge ahụ; a na-edebe oke ya na 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                         | Tọọ "false" iji kwụsị inyocha host n'ezie nke provider e kenyere proxy (`GET /models`, enweghị API key) ma jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile kama ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), nyocha nnweta akpaghị aka (scheduler + bọtịnụ "Nwalee Ha Niile" nke `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) bụ **naanị-ọgụgụ** ma ọ dịghị mgbe ha na-ede status proxy — naanị onye njikwa na-ahọpụta active/inactive, ka nyocha na-adịghị akwụsi ike ghara ime ka proxy e kenyere gharazi ịrụ ọrụ (#6246). Tọọ `true` iji weghachite omume ochie nke nnwale-na-ntọala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                   | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ isonye na ya (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya sitere na DB dashboard na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anwalegharị ntụgharị streaming atụgharịrị nke tọgbọ chakoo site n'ụzọ credential nkịtị (ruo nnwale ọzọ `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX`) kama igosipụta 200 tọgbọ chakoo ma ọ bụ 502 nwere ọdịnaya tọgbọ chakoo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                   | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ isonye na ya (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya sitere na DB dashboard na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-akwadebe nyocha ọdịnaya stream tọgbọ chakoo nke Claude/Codex ma jiri nnwale ọzọ dị ugbu a nke nwere oke na connection ọzọ tozuru etozu mgbe ntụgharị `empty_content` gasịrị, na-enweghị cooldown ma ọ bụ mkpọchi model.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                   | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ isonye na ya (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya sitere na DB dashboard na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-aga n'ihu iwere sample nke otu chunk e zigara n'ime 16 mgbe 64 mbụ gasịrị, ka output a na-ahụ anya nke bịara n'oge ka nwee ike ịka stream ahụ dịka nke wepụtala output (otu 5xx nke bịara mgbe output gasịrị ga-anọgide naanị n'ogo request kama ịbụ mkpọchi naanị-model).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                   | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ a na-ahọrọ iji mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nnọchi nke DB dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nlele egress nke pool nke bụ naanị maka ịgụ n'okpuru proxy pool dị na dashboard (adreesị IP egress dị iche iche, njikọ, na nke a kacha ahụ n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, site na ndekọ proxy). A naghị eji ya eme routing ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                                   | Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ a na-ahọrọ iji mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nnọchi nke DB dashboard ga-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anabata adreesị ndị a hụrụ nke operator zigara, ndị nwere ụbọchị maka onye ọ bụla so na pool, ma jikọta ha na ihe a gụrụ na journal maka ngosipụta na nhazi pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                                  | Oge nlele n'ime awa maka ahịrị egress ndị operator zigara (a na-agụ ya n'oge ọgụgụ ọ bụla; ọ dịghị mkpa ịmalitegharịa). E debere oke ya na 1..168; uru karịrị oke, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ nke tọgbọ chakoo ga-alaghachi na 24, tinyere otu ịdọ aka ná ntị naanị maka usoro ahụ dum, ọ gaghị atụpụ njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Tọọ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler wepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka mgbe ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha mere ugboro ugboro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha tupu scheduler ewepụ proxy na-akpaghị aka (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Tọọ `true` iji kwe ka scheduler gbanyụọ proxy n'ụzọ dị nro (ọnọdụ `dead`, anaghị ehichapụ ya ma ọlị) mgbe ọdịda ndị na-esochi ibe ha mere ugboro ugboro, kama iwepụ ya. Ọ bụ nhọrọ ọzọ na-adịghị emebi ihe n'ọnọdụ `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: a na-ewepụ proxy ozugbo na mkpebi pool/rotation (ihe nzacha ọnọdụ dị ndụ nke mkpebi scope-pool ji eme ihe ewepụlarị ya), a ga-emekwa ka ọ rụọ ọrụ ọzọ na-akpaghị aka ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe ule probe ọzọ. Ọ na-eji otu oke `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na ọkọlọtọ abụọ ahụ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ga-emeri.                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                                   | Nhọrọ passive skip: mgbe ọ bụ `true`, scheduler na-amafere probe dị ndụ maka endpoint nke okporo ụzọ production ya na nso nso a gosipụtarala na ọ dị mma (ọ dịkarịa ala otu ihe ịga nke ọma na enweghị ọdịda proxy e kenyere ya n'ime windo ahụ, ma hụ naanị otu provider). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara: a na-anwale proxy ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                              | Ogologo windo n'ime ms nke okporo ụzọ production passive skip na-enyocha (e debere oke ya na `60000..599999`). Ọ dị mkpụmkpụ karịa nkeji sweep nkeji 10 ka ọdịda mere na nso nso a wee bụrụ nke kacha emetụta nsonaazụ kama ka a belata mmetụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                                     | Kwe ka usoro OAuth na nkwado provider gafee proxy e kpọgidere ma jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nyocha mbido nke ikike iru proxy dara. Usoro mkpa dị irè bụ ntọala nnọchi nke Feature Flags DB > env var > ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)            | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                                     | Atụmatụ oge **ichere n'ahịrị** ndabara: ogologo oge arịrịọ nwere ike ichere oghere onye na-eweta ma nọrọ n'ahịrị tupu a jụ ya, na-eruteghị upstream ma ọlị. A na-akwụsị ngụoge ahụ ozugbo ọrụ ahụ malitere ịrụ — `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS` na-amachi oge mmezu ya n'ụzọ dị iche. Env na-edobe naanị ndabara: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` echekwara, emesịa `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` nke njikọ ọ bụla; nke ọ bụla n'ime ha na-aka ya mkpa.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (nkeji 10)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                              | Oke ogologo oge arịrịọ anabatara nwere ike ịnọ na mmezu tupu ndokwa rate-limit ya kubie ume — e kewapụrụ ya na atụmatụ oge ichere n'ahịrị ka mmalite fetch dị nwayọ na gateway ndị na-abụghị incremental ghara ịkwụsị n'ihi oge agwụla (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (agbanyụrụ)          | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                              | Oke nnabata n'ahịrị: jiri 429 `queue_full` jụ arịrịọ ozugbo ọnụọgụ arịrịọ ndị nọrịrị n'ahịrị ruru nke a. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(edobeghị)_             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                              | Manye nchekwa rate limit nke na-agbanye onwe ya ka ọ gbanye/gbanyụọ n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard echekwara (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji manye ya ịgbanye, yana `false`/`0`/`off` iji manye ya ịgbanyụọ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ (ndabara), a na-echebe njikọ API-key ndị na-arụ ọrụ site na rate limit na-akpaghị aka. Iji `rateLimitProtection: false` hazie njikọ ọ bụla na-agbanyụọ nchebe akọwapụtara, mana ọ naghị ewepụ njikọ ahụ na nchekwa a; ịhazi env var a ka ọ bụrụ `false` bụ usoro zuru ụwa ọnụ iji mee ka njikọ API-key rụọ ọrụ n'enweghị ahịrị rate-limit.                                          |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(edobeghị → agbanyụrụ)_ | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                       | Nsochi cooldown zuru ụwa ọnụ gafee arịrịọ dị iche iche maka onye na-eweta/njikọ, nke a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara (ọ na Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker arụkọ ọrụ ụfọdụ). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji gbanye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                       | Cooldown kacha nta (ms) tupu a nwalee onye na-eweta/njikọ dara ada ọzọ. Ọ na-abawanye n'ụdị exponential dabere na ọdịda ndị sochiri onwe ha. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (nkeji 5)       | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                                       | Oke cooldown kacha elu (ms) tupu a nwalee onye na-eweta/njikọ dara ada ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(edobeghị → agbanyụrụ)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ ntọala) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro ọrụ) | **Gịnị:** mgbake doro anya nke stream upstream ndị e gbupụrụ (port free-claude-code). Ọ na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ka nkwụsị _tupu commit_ — nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla erute onye ahịa — meghee ọzọ ma nwalee ya ọzọ n'enweghị onye ahịa ịhụ ya. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** maka upstream ndị na-adịghị akwụsi ike/ndị na-egbutukarị stream na 0 byte mgbe ọ na-amalite; hapụ ya ka ọ bụrụ agbanyụrụ ma ọ bụrụ na ịnweghị ike ịnagide mgbakwunye oge ruru 750 ms tupu token mbụ apụta na stream ọ bụla. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Ọ na-akụ mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience echekwara; ntọala Dashboard na-aka ya mkpa ozugbo edobere ya. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(edobeghị → agbanyụrụ)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (usoro arụmọrụ)    | **Gịnị:** ịga n'ihu n'etiti stream (Usoro 4.4) — mgbe mbelata _post-commit_ gasịrị (bytes erutela onye ahịa), zipụ arịrịọ ọzọ site n'iji ederede ezughị ezu dị ka ndejupụta mbụ nke assistant, wee jikọta suffix na-efu. Ọ bụ naanị maka stream ederede nkịtị dakọtara na OpenAI; ọ naghị arụ ọrụ ma ọ bụrụ na tool call ka na-aga n'ihu. **Mgbe a ga-agbanye ya:** maka mmepụta ogologo ndị a na-akwụsị n'etiti azịza ma ọ bụrụ na ị nabatara ka ọdụ ederede eweghachiri abịa n'otu oge kama token n'otu n'otu. Ọ nọọrọ onwe ya na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe ize ndụ dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                       |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(edobeghị → agbanyụrụ)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                         | Watchdog mmepụta bara uru nke stream na-arụ ọrụ, nke a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye. Ọ na-achọpụta stream ndị na-aga n'ihu izipu chunks mana na-anọ n'okpuru ọsọ mmepụta assistant ahaziri; heartbeats, ihe omume ojiji, deltas efu, na usoro tool/reasoning anaghị eme ka a ga-asị na e nwere ọganihu. Ọ dị iche na oge nkwụsị n'ihi enweghị ọrụ na oge njedebe siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                           | Oge amara tupu nyocha throughput amalite, nke oke ya bụ 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                           | Window mmepụta bara uru na-agagharị, nke oke ya bụ 1000–600000 ms; a chọrọ otu window zuru ezu tupu ịkwụsị ọrụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                           | Ọsọ byte UTF-8 kacha nta nke mmepụta assistant (nnọchi token e ji nlezianya eme atụmatụ), nke oke ya bụ 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                           | Nlele mmepụta bara uru kacha nta nke karịrị efu a na-ewere dị ka nke a pụrụ ịtụ, nke oke ya bụ 1–1000000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                        | Etiti oge stagger (ms) n'etiti nyocha ahụike token nke provider n'oge mmalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                        | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha nta (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nyocha ahụike token nke provider, iji gbochie izipu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                        | Jitter enweghị usoro kacha ukwuu (ms) a na-agbakwunye n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nyocha ahụike token nke provider, iji gbochie izipu ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge (Nsogbu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                        | Ogo batch maka nyocha ndị na-eme n'otu oge n'ime nyocha token-healthcheck nke mmalite; ụkpụrụ ndị ka ukwuu na-enyocha ọtụtụ njikọ n'otu oge, ebe ụkpụrụ ndị ka nta na-ebelata ibu arịrịọ mberede (Nsogbu #7875, nlọghachi nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                             | Ọnụọgụ nnwale ọzọ akpaka a na-eme maka nzaghachi cooldown metụtara model tupu eweghachiri onye ahịa njehie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                             | Oke kachasị nke oge nchere na-abawanye (sekọnd) n'etiti nnwale ọzọ mgbe oge nkwụsị gasịrị. Uru a ga-amachi ya n'agbanyeghị `Retry-After` sitere na upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                         | URL proxy Headroom na-echekwa token. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-ebido CLI `headroom-ai` mpaghara na loopback na ndabara; gbanwee ya naanị iji tụọ aka na proxy sidecar Docker mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Konstantị nhazi mgbake stream

A na-eji konstantị e dere kpọmkwem na koodu dị na
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`) ahazi omume njide nke mgbake, dịka e gosiri ebe a maka ntụaka —
ịgbanwe ha chọrọ idezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — ogologo oge a na-ejide windo SSE mmeghe
  ka e nwee ike ịnwale ọzọ ma ọ bụrụ na ebipụ ya n'oge tupu e ziga byte ọ bụla n'aka klayentị.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — oke siri ike maka windo ahụ a na-ejide; kwado
  (flush + passthrough) ozugbo byte ole a ruru, n'agbanyeghị timer.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — ọnụọgụ kachasị nke imepegharị stream upstream
  n'ụzọ na-adịghị ahụ anya mgbe njide ahụ ka akwadobeghị.

> **Oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla (enweghị env var):** _fallback_
> oke ọnụego sliding-window maka provider ọ bụla ebufere site na FCC dị na koodu
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, nke ejikọtara site na
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana a na-ebunye ya na **map ndabara efu**
> ma ọ **nweghị env var maka onye njikwa sistemụ** ugbu a — a na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ naanị site na hook ule /
> ndezi koodu. E kpachapụrụ anya ahapụ ya na tebụl dị n'elu. Limiter relay maka `(token, IP)`
> nke _nwere_ ihe njikwa bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Netwọk & Ports).
