## 22. Nchọpụta na ndozi nsogbu

> [!CAUTION]
> Ngbanwe ndị a na-emepụta **mmepụta zuru ezu** ma nwee ike ikpughe data dị nzuzo. **Agbanyela ha mgbe ọ bụla na gburugburu mmepụta.**

| Ngbanwe                               | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji gbanye ndekọ zuru ezu nke Cursor executor (iberibe SSE a tụgharịrị, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Aha ọzọ nke `CURSOR_DEBUG` nke dakọtara na nsụgharị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-anata iberibe Cursor raw a tụgharịrị mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge njedebe nke stream mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge mgbaghara (ms) mgbe composer kv_after_text soft terminator gasịrị ma ọ bụrụ na byte ka dị na buffer — nke a na-enye oku ngwaọrụ exec_mcp nke na-esote oge iji mezue frame ya.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | agbanyere (`!== "0"`)                      | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ntuziaka tool-commit nke na-eme ka composer-2.5 kpọọ ngwaọrụ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịdabere na ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Ederede system prompt nke onye ọrụ sistemụ kọwara, nke a na-agbakwunye na system message MGBE ntụgharị gasịrị (ntinye mgbe ntụgharị gasịrị), ma rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka mbido ntuziaka a na-ewepụ na ngọngọ system preamble ndị e weghachiri. Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                            | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Tọọ `1` iji wepụ ngọngọ system-prompt preamble ndị e weghachiri site na mmalite nke stream openai→claude. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — usoro nyocha ndị ahụ dabere n'ụdị ederede Bekee ma na-agbanwe payload nzaghachi, ya mere nzaghachi nke malitere n'ụzọ ziri ezi site na ngalaba dị otu ahụ ga-efunahụ ya.                  |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                                    | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Oge njedebe nbudata maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ntinye ọhụhụ `image_url` dị n'ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(a na-enyocha)_                           | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Dochie ọchụchọ Cursor IDE state DB a na-eji achọpụta nsụgharị IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(chọpụta / kpọgide)_                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | NJ build nke Agent CLI (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) maka `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` na Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_BIN`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ụzọ gaa na Cursor Agent binary a na-eji emepụta onyonyo. Mgbe edoghị ya, handler na-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, mesịa PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CURSOR_IMG_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `210000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor Agent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MAX_CONCURRENT`           | `2`                                        | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ụzọ njikwa ọrụ na-aga n'otu oge n'oche a na-ekekọrịta maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MODEL`                    | arịrịọ / `auto`                            | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Dochie `--model` nke Cursor CLI maka ọrụ onyonyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                                    | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `3000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_REQUEST_SHAPE`         | agbanyụrụ (a ga-ahọrọ iji `"1"` gbanye ya) | `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`                                                            | Dekọọ akara content-type/length maka payload nkata buru ibu mgbe atọrọ `"1"`. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji belata mkpọtụ ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEBUG_RESPONSES_SSE_TO_JSON`         | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji dekọọ nkọwa ntụgharị Responses API SSE→JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DEBUG_CLAUDE_NONSTREAM`              | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji gosipụta iberibe textContent efu n'ụzọ ntụgharị nzaghachi Claude (naanị maka nchọpụta nsogbu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_E2E_MODE`      | _(edoghị ya)_                              | Ngwa nnwale E2E                                                                                       | Tọọ `true` iji gbanye ọnọdụ nnwale E2E (nkwenye e mere ka ọ dị mfe, njikọ nnwale).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 23. Njikọta GitHub

Kwe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkọ nsogbu ozugbo site na Dashboard.

| Ihe Mgbanwe           | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | GitHub Personal Access Token nwere ikike `issues:write`.                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(edobeghị)_ | nhazi mbido nke nsogbu / ihe enyemaka cloud agent | Token nnweta GitHub izugbe a na-eji dị ka ndabere maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, nke ihe enyemaka cloud agent dị na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` na-ejikwa. |

---

## Ọnọdụ Mbuga

Maka ntuziaka SRE nke backend relay (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe a ga-edowe 9router ma e jiri ya tụnyere CLIProxyAPI, na atụmatụ ndabere maka oke ọsọ mbufe), lee [Atụmatụ Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dịkarịsịrị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Enweghị Njikọ Ịntanetị / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Proxy Ntụgharị (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Ebe Nnwale Nchekwa Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na njikwa nchekwa ndị a na-etinye mgbe usoro Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme akpaaka ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ maka nchekwa.

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                  | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge elekere mgbidi maka koodu skill ọ bụla a na-eme n'ebe nnwale nchekwa. Nke a bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(na-alaghachi na `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge nhazi skill n'ọkwa dị elu. Tọọ ya ka ọ dị elu karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụọgụ bytes kachasị nke skill nwere ike ịgụ site na otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị n'ebe nnwale nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụọgụ bytes kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike maka ọnụọgụ mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachi site na otu oku ebe nnwale nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                         | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọkụ na-apụ apụ site n'ime ebe nnwale nchekwa. Ndabara ya bụ **nkewapụrụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                         | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta onyonyo container ndị e kwere ka e jiri mee ihe n'ebe nnwale nchekwa, nke rikoma kewapụtara. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ pụtara na ọ bụ naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                      | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Onyonyo container a na-eji mgbe a na-amalite ebe nnwale nchekwa nke Docker na-akwado. Gbanwee ya iji kegide onyonyo ntọala omenala e wusiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                          | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka idowe skill n'ebe nnwale nchekwa: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kacha mma dabere na OS nke host ahụ (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma laghachi na Docker ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ime ka `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` rụọ ọrụ na-emepe ụzọ ọpụpụ site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana iwu `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ebe nrụnye ndị mmadụ na-ekekọrịta.

## 25. Oke Ojiji Ndị Na-eweta Ọrụ, Ọwara Netwọkụ, Nkwado Ndabere & Ihe Runtime Ndị Ọzọ

Endpoint oke ojiji ndị na-eweta ọrụ, ọwara netwọkụ (Tailscale, Ngrok, proxy ndozi MITM), ọdọ egress 1Proxy, nkwado ndabere nchekwa data na obere mgbanwe ndị akọwapụtara maka atụmatụ ọ bụla nke layer executor ma ọ bụ script na-eji.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend nke ihe mgbochi ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host mmepụta)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Gbanwee host eji enweta oke coding-plan Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | ewepụtara site na host                                                      | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Mgbanwe URL zuru ezu nke oke maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Kuki nnọkọ console maka ụzọ mbata oke Token Plan nkeonwe nke Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (igodo API inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo header arịrịọ `Cookie` niile — ọ nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` — site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla gaa na `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Ịgba Ụgwọ › Ndebanye Aha (F12 › Netwọkụ). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma bụrụ naanị maka nnọkọ ahụ; ọ ka mma iji oghere Dashboard `qwenCloudCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                       |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe `sec_token` ejiri aka tinye maka ụzọ mbata console Token Plan. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe edobeghị ya, fetcher na-eji kuki ahụ chọpụta ya site na HTML dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Mgbanwe host nke ụzọ mbata maka fetcher oke Token Plan nkeonwe (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL dashboard eji achọpụta `sec_token` site na HTML onye ọrụ banyere na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Kuki nnọkọ console maka API oke Token Plan Xiaomi MiMo (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — igodo API inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo header arịrịọ `Cookie` niile — ọ nwere `api-platform_serviceToken` na `userId` — site na arịrịọ ọ bụla gaa na `platform.xiaomimimo.com` na ibe Token Plan (F12 › Netwọkụ). Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo ma bụrụ naanị maka nnọkọ ahụ; ọ ka mma iji oghere Dashboard `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                             |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Mmefu ego kwa ọnwa (Credits) maka Xiaomi MiMo ndabere nke OmniRoute na-esochi, nke a na-eji mgbe ahazighị kuki console. Ndabara ya bụ 4.1B nke ọkwa Lite; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ Credits nke atụmatụ ahụ (Standard 11B / Pro 38B / Max 82B). Ntọgharị `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` nke njikọ ọ bụla na-ebute ụzọ karịa env var a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Ntọgharị ụzọ front-end nke console maka iwepụta oke vision/media nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Ntọgharị ụzọ front-end nke console maka iwepụta oke multimodal nke ọkwa efu Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(profaịlụ ụyọkọ a na-ahọrọ iji)_                                         | Izi ezi nke imepụta ndeksi HNSW. Ọnụọgụ dị elu = nrụpụta dị nwayọ, ọchụchọ dị ngwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ntụgharị akaụntụ nke onye njikwa nwere ike ịhazi. Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime klaasị `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` dị n'okpuru na-akpalite ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ (ọnọdụ mgbanyụ-ukwu ahụ na-egbochikwa klaasị 429/500/502 ndị agbanyere na ndabara). Ọ na-enye ohere ka ihu-ngwa nlekọta (dịka ngwa desktọpụ VibeProxy) detuo iwu ntụgharị nke ya n'ime injin ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ nke backend.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge nchere (sekọnd) a na-etinye n'akaụntụ e tinyere oke arịrịọ mgbe ọrụ dị n'elu enyeghị ntụziaka doro anya maka nrụgharị. `0` = jiri oge nchere ndabara nke injin kama uru edoziri iji dochie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oyiri nke mmasị ihu-ngwa “akpanyela akara dị ka nke e tinyere oke arịrịọ ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị oge nrụgharị”.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Na-enyere ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ aka maka njehie ọnọdụ `429`. Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ma `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ, kama a na-eweghachiri ya onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `429` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo, na-echekwa omume e ji mara usoro ahụ kemgbe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windọ na-akpụ akpụ (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `429` n'ime ya iji ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Na-enyere ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ aka maka njehie sava `5xx` (ewepụ `502`, nke nwere klaasị nke ya). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, njehie ndị a anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `5xx` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `5xx` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ntọala nkwado ndabere dabere na steeti iji mee ihe maka njehie `502` (ọnụ ụzọ na-adịghị mma). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, `502` anaghịzi akpalite ịgbanwe akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `502` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a gbanwee akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-agbanwe ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `502` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nhọrọ a ga-ahọrọ iji rụọ ọrụ (ndabara bụ ỌGBANYỤ): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, `400` nkịtị (arịrịọ na-adịghị mma) na-akpalitekwa ịgbanwe akaụntụ. Nke a na-agbakwụnye omume naanị — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-egbochi omume dị adị nke injin ahụ ebe `400` nwere ederede gbasara oke ojiji/koota ka na-agafe gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `400` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a gbanwee akaụntụ ahụ (a na-elele nke a naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-amị amị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `400` n'ime ya maka iru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Onye Nhazi Nkwadebe Claude

Nkwadebe Cron na-achịkwa maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị họọrọ isonye, ka obere arịrịọ ahaziri n'oge meghee windo oke ojiji nke awa 5 kama ịbụ arịrịọ izizi n'ezie ga-eme ya (#8848). Onye nhazi ahụ anaghị arụ ọrụ ma ọ bụrụ na `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` abụghị eziokwu **yana** edere njikọ ahụ na `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na ọ dịghị ihe a ga-akwadebe ọbụlagodi mgbe env var ahụ dị ọkụ.

| Agbanwe                        | Ndabara                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(edoghị → gbanyụrụ)_       | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ihe nhazi warmup. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ọ naghị ele mkpụrụedemede ukwu ma ọ bụ nta anya, a na-ewepụ oghere). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ga-ahapụ ihe nhazi ahụ ka ọ gbanyụrụ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nkwupụta cron nwere oghere ise maka tick warmup, nke a na-enyocha na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge nrụgharị Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere host.                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọnụọgụ njikọ a na-eme warmup n'otu oge n'ime tick ọ bụla. A na-amachibido ya n'etiti `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-alaghachi na `3`.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model a na-eji maka arịrịọ warmup. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị na atụmatụ gị; họrọ model kachasị ọnụ ala nke ka nwere ike imepe window ahụ.                                                                    |

### Oge VNC Maka Nbanye Ihe Nchọgharị & Utuaha Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC etinyere n'ime container nke a na-eji ejide ozi nzere site na nbanye ihe nchọgharị mmekọrịta (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere utuaha `DATA_DIR` ochie. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara VNC na-ezo aka na image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` e tinyere n'ime ngwugwu, a na-agbanwekwa ha naanị maka image container ahaziri iche, port, ma ọ bụ nhazi okirikiri ndụ.

| Agbanwe                               | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tag image Docker maka container nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ mee ka nke a zoo aka na image ahaziri iche.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binary runtime container a na-eji ebido container VNC (dịka ọmụmaatụ, tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `podman`).                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC/noVNC e kpughere n'ime container.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ directory profaịlụ Chromium dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Directory host nke na-edobe profaịlụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị echekwara.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị n'ihi enweghị ọrụ (ms) tupu ewepụ oge VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) maka ogologo ndụ otu oge VNC.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke oge VNC nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ichere ka ihe nchọgharị dị n'ime container dị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka iwepụta oge/cookies e jidere mgbe nbanye zuru ezu.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Flags command-line ndị ọzọ a na-enyefe Chromium dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche nke container nbanye VNC na-esonye (#12571) kama bridge ndabara, ka containers ibe ya ghara iru port bridge CDP ya.                                                   |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edoghị)_                                                               | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Utuaha ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị ma `DATA_DIR` ma `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`. Ọ na-achọta cache oge web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Nnwale & E2E Harness

Ndị `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji ya. Ahapụla
uru niile dị n'okpuru ka edoghị ha na mbupu mmepụta.

| Mgbanwe                                | Ndabara                            | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| -------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ụdị mbido E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-alaghachi na `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe nchịkwa a na-etinye n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ nyocha ahụike mpaghara a na-eme ugboro ugboro mgbe Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ okirikiri nyocha ahụike token OAuth n'oge nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(edoghị)_                         | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-eweta ọrụ e ji rikoma kewaa nke ewepụrụ na nyocha imegharị token nke a na-eme tupu nsogbu apụta (dịka `codex,openai`). Ọ bụ ụzọ ọzọ ezubere iche kama ịgbanyụ nyocha ahụike kpamkpam — ndị na-eweta ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpụmkpụ na-aga n'ihu na-emegharị, ebe ndị na-eweta cascade na-eme naanị mgbe ihe mere. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ ndekọ nyocha ahụike na stdout Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mafere nrụpụta mmepụta Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (nkwalite CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Mafere hook iwepụ OmniRoute (CI na-eji ya edowe `node_modules` ka ọ ghara ịgbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                           | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge nchere (ms) ka sava nwee ahụike tupu e mee nnwale ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`     | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke Electron smoke harness na-atụ anya ka ngwa a kwakọtara nye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ngụkọta oge ngwụcha (ms) tupu smoke harness kwụsị ịnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge nkwụsị (ms) mgbe peeji ahụ buchara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(akpaghị aka)_                    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke ọma gaa na executable Electron a kwakọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ndekọ data maka ịgba nnwale smoke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data smoke mgbe ịgba ọsọ ahụ gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji ziga ndekọ Electron na stdout ozugbo n'oge ịgba ọsọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: jiri otu ndekọ data ahụ malitegharị ya ma chọpụta na mmalite nke abụọ họọrọ ọkwọ SQLite nke sistemụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọgharị PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Dochie ụzọ faịlụ binary nke Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Usoro nsụgharị dọkụmentị

`scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji ya (iwu `npm run i18n:run`).
Agbanweela isii ndị a niile ka ha ghara inwe uru na ndabara — tọọ ha na `.env` naanị na kọmputa
ndị kwesịrị inwe ike ịgba onye ntụgharị dọkụmentị ahụ.

| Agbanwe                                  | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi                                | Nkọwa                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`          | _(edoghị ya)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | URL ntọala dakọtara na OpenAI maka backend nsụgharị.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`          | _(edoghị ya)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Token Bearer maka backend nsụgharị (anaghị edekọ ya ma ọlị).                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`            | _(edoghị ya)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | NJ model, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `60000`       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Oge ngwụcha maka arịrịọ ọ bụla n'ime milisekọnd.                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT` | _(edoghị ya)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Njikwa ntụgharị uche nhọrọ, nke a na-eziga na backend otu ọ dị mgbe edobere ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`      | `4`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Arịrịọ nsụgharị ndị na-aga n'otu oge mgbe a na-arụ ọrụ n'ọtụtụ faịlụ / locale.  |

## 27. Nri Radar (Nnabata n'Onwe Gị)

Mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED na-achịkwa (agbanyụrụ na ndabara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara
a na-agbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Ngbanwe anọ mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ iji dochie ntọala maka nri a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara,
yana usoro igodo ndị nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị uru ndabara nke na-eduga na
ogwe ọrụ nzuzo nke onye nwe ya. Lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka akwụkwọ zuru ezu
banyere modul ahụ na
[usoro ịgbalite ya site na mmalite ruo na njedebe na nhazi a na-eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

A naghị eji environment variable ahazi ngwa ọgụgụ ọkwa Home/Changelog izugbe,
ọ dịghịkwa adabere na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-eji naanị GET agụ URL `news.json`
nke repository ọha, nke akọwapụtara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts`;
ID nke ihe a jụrụ ka dị na nchekwa mpaghara nke ihe nchọgharị.

| Variable                      | Ndabara                                      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL ntọala nke katalọgụ, ntụnye, onyinye ndị nkwado, na nri Intel ndị a bịanyere aka na ha iche iche na-ekekọrịta. Dochie ya ka ọ na-atụ aka na ọrụ a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara e debere kpọmkwem)_          | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Igodo ọha Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) e ji enyocha mbinye aka nri sitere na nri ahaziri iche.                                                                                            |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye nyere aka" na-emepe (usoro ịnata igodo onye nkwado site na GitHub OAuth).                                                                                     |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Kwado ọrụ ahụ" na-emepe (ibe ịkwụ ụgwọ/atụmatụ).                                                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ nzuzo nke ogwe ọrụ maka naanị onye nwe ya. A chọrọ HTTPS, ma e wezụga maka nnyefe SSH loopback nke HTTP; uru edoghị ma ọ bụ nke na-ezighi ezi anaghị emepụta ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla.           |

---

## Nnyocha: Variable E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghịzi Arụ Ọrụ

Variable ndị a pụtara na ụdị `.env.example` ndị gara aga, mana ha **enweghị ntụaka runtime ọ bụla** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụla ha:

| Mgbanwe                                                                                                                                                                         | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla anaghị agụ ya. SQLite bụ naanị draịva a na-akwado — ọ dịghị mkpa ịhọrọ nke ọ bụla.                                    |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Ọ dị na dọkụmentị ochie/ụkpụrụ env mana anaghị eji ya n'oge arụmọrụ. Ọ nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ-instance n'ọdịnihu.              |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. E nweghị mmachi e ji aka tinye n'ogo nchekwa data.                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụla nhicha faịlụ ochie.                                                           |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị faịlụ CLI binary).                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | E zoghị aka na ha ebe ọ bụla n'ime koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                          |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ ha na v3.8.0. Runtime anaghịzi agụ env vars ndị a — nzere na-abịa site na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Kiro dị na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — toggle ya enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.                                         |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na stubs API key ndị ọzọ anaghị eji eme ihe na v3.8.0.                                                                              |

### Ndozi Uru Ndabara

| Mgbanwe                   | Uru `.env.example` Ochie | Ndabara Koodu N'ezie | Ihe Edoziri                                                   |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |

### Mmegharị config OpenCode (ngwaọrụ ad-hoc)

`scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji ya emegharị `opencode.json`
nwere uru `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi, nke e wetara site na instance
OmniRoute na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ndị a achọrọ maka arụmọrụ nkịtị — script a
bụ naanị ngwaọrụ ndị mmepe.

| Mgbanwe            | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | URL ntọala nke instance OmniRoute a ga-ajụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API maka nkwenye njirimara megide endpoint OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OPENCODE_API_KEY`.            |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) nke a na-ede n'ime `opencode.json` e mepụtaghachiri. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OMNIROUTE_KEY`. |

### Ngwa nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị (ngwa ad-hoc)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị, na-eji ya.
Achọghị ya maka ọrụ nkịtị — ọ bụ naanị ngwa ndị nrụpụta.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Ozi nzere JSON nke onye njikwa nyere maka provider nke CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị na-anwale (a na-eji `JSON.parse` atụgharị ya). Hapụ ya n'edoghị maka nnwale na-enweghị mmejuputa. |

### Oge Nchọgharị VNC

`src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji ha ahazi oge Chromium na-enweghị interface eserese nke dabere na Docker maka ndị provider akpaaka nchọgharị. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — e gosiri ndabara n'okpuru.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi                                   | Nkọwa                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na binary Docker e ji emepụta container VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Image Docker maka container VNC Chromium.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(flags arụnyere n'ime)_       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Arụmụka CLI Chromium ndị ọzọ a na-ebufe na nchọgharị dị n'ime container.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC dị n'ime container.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ profaịlụ dị n'ime container.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị n'akụkụ host maka profaịlụ nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe ịnọ nkịtị (ms) tupu a chịkọta oge VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị nke session (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ session VNC kachasị nwere ike ịrụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchọgharị ịdị njikere (ms).                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchịkọta/nhicha (ms).                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche container na-esonyere (#12571), kama bridge ndabara. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Ndekọ maka nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke session eriri Notion.               |

### Nkwenye njirimara ọrụ ime

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Token inline maka nkwenye njirimara site n'otu ọrụ gaa n'ọzọ na management plane.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nwere token ọrụ ime (ọ ka mma n'ime container; ọ na-akagbu mgbanwe inline). |

### Ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara   | Nkọwa                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mafere iweta ọnụọgụ OpenRouter nke provider ọ bụla maka ime ka katalọgụ baa ọgaranya. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL cache (ms) maka ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter e wetara.                                                           |

### Njikọ Redis agbakwunyere n'ime

| Mgbanwe                     | Ndabara     | Nkọwa                                                               |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adreesị njikọ maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                          |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Ntọgharị nwere oke OmniRoute maka adreesị njikọ Redis agbakwunyere. |
