## 24. Ihe agbakwunyere na mbipụta v3.8.50

E webatara ntọala ndị a mgbe e mechara foto ọnọdụ nkwekọrịta gburugburu ebe obibi gara aga.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                    | Faịlụ Isi Mbido                                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oge kachasị a ga-echere oghere nnabata nkata dị arọ tupu `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ apụta; obere oge nchere nwere oke na-ahazi njupụta arịrịọ ndị nnọchi anya n'usoro kama iwepụta `503` ozugbo. `0` na-eweghachi ọjụjụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Atụmatụ oke baịtị ndị e debere n'ahịrị maka nchere nnabata: ọ na-amachi mkpokọta baịtị body echekwara nwa oge n'ofe usoro ahụ dum ka nchere ghara ịba ụba heap (#4380). Nchere ndị gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ọ bụzịghị ihe a na-akwado ma ọ naghị eme ihe kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke nnọkọ ọ bụla ma jiri otu atụmatụ oke maka usoro ahụ dum dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ọ bụzịghị ihe a na-akwado ma ọ naghị eme ihe kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ ụzọ nnabata nke nnọkọ ọ bụla ma jiri otu atụmatụ oke maka usoro ahụ dum dochie ha. A na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (agbanyụrụ)            | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Ụzọ nnabata mebere nke runtime na-emegharị onwe ya (#9654): isi mgba ọkụ maka ọnụ ụzọ na-emegharị onwe ya nke tenant ọ bụla (sistemụ 2). Ọ dị iche na mgbanwe ụzọ nke njikọ ọ bụla a kwụsịrị ịkwado dị n'elu (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, ndị anaghị eme ihe kemgbe #10110). Ọ bụ ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ dashboard nwere otu aha ahụ; env var na-aka nhazi dashboard e ji dochie ya; ọ chọrọ ịmalitegharị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Na-amachi ogologo oge oku run-now ga-echere ọrụ na-aga n'ihu tupu ọ malite ọrụ e debere n'ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ site na nnọkọ Chrome CDP nke akaụntụ ọ bụla; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-echekwa nnọkọ Adobe ndị a rụziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`; tọọ `0` maka ọnọdụ ebe nchekwa naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oghere oge kacha nta n'etiti izipu arịrịọ mmepụta Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oge ezumike ọzọ mgbe izipu arịrịọ Adobe nke ọma nke ugboro atọ ọ bụla gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Ọnọdụ true-headless maka nbipu njehie naanị; Adobe colligo na-ajụkarị nnọkọ ihe ize ndụ si na ya pụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | nchọpụta-akpaka            | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Faịlụ Chrome executable zuru oke enwere ike ịhọrọ, nke ndị executor ihe nchọgharị na-eji mgbe nchọpụta ikpo okwu akpaka ezughị oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(edobeghị)_               | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token BotFather nke na-eme ka webhook mbata rụọ ọrụ ma na-abịanye aka na Mini App `initData`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(edobeghị)_               | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Nzuzo nkekọrịta e debanyere site na `setWebhook` ma kwado ya megide header `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` na nnyefe webhook ọ bụla. A chọrọ ya maka ụzọ webhook; ma ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, a ga-eji 503 jụ nnyefe webhook.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model eji aza nkata Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Ndochi URL ntọala Bot API maka proxy ma ọ bụ sava Bot API a na-akwado n'onwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Oge ngwụcha n'ime milisekọnd maka oku Bot API na-apụ apụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (agbanyere)            | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Tọọ `0` ka a ghara imepụta tarball `.tar.gz` mgbe a na-akwadebe ngwugwu ML/ihe nchọgharị nhọrọ maka osisi Electron standalone (a ka na-emepụta ndekọ ngwugwu na `optional-packs.index.json`). Usoro ọrụ mbipụta desktop na-eji ya belata nha artifact a na-ebugo.                                                                                                                              |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED`          | `false`                    | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`                                                       | Na-eme ka ndebanye ụzọ onyonyo ndenye aha xAI rụọ ọrụ; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka ụzọ onyonyo API-key ghara ịgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                    | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Oge izu ike maka key ọ bụla n'ime milisekọnd mgbe nzaghachi `429` enweghị ntụnye `Retry-After` bara uru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`           | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts`                                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off`, `0`, ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ ndekọ na ojiji nhazi estimator n'ime ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(n'ime sistemụ)_          | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Ndochi signal-handler nke sava standalone dị n'ime sistemụ; launcher na-edobe ma na-ehichapụkarị ya kama ndị operator ịhazi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Ntọala ndabara zuru ụwa ọnụ maka gburugburu oge nrụọrụ ihe nchọgharị na ọwara ngwaọrụ na-apụ apụ. Uru njikọ ahaziri na dashboard na-ebute ụzọ.

| Ngbanwe                                 | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara maka nrụnye npm, systemd na PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Nhọrọ ndabere nkịtị maka ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Ebe njedebe CDP dị n'ime; Docker na-eji sidecar dị na ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(edobeghị — agbanyụrụ)_       | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`                   | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` (ma hazie ebe njedebe CDP) iji jiri profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị a na-ejikwa site n'èzí, nke abanyelarị, na ibe ChatGPT ya dị ugbu a kama iji context dịpụrụ iche n'oge ntụgharị ọ bụla; ọ na-amachikwa taabụ ChatGPT ndị nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge ka ha bụrụ 1. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara. Hụ [profaịlụ CDP na-adịgide adịgide](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                  | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                                | Nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta nke a chọrọ n'ime nkụnyeisi `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` maka arịrịọ HTTP ọ bụla nke proxy CDP na nkwalite WebSocket. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, proxy ahụ na-ebunye ma ọ bụ na-emepụta token site n'iji `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN_FILE`; ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị token a pụrụ iji, ọ ga-ajụ arịrịọ niile. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | ID tunnel OpenAI zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ntụgharị ngwá ọrụ Codex mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Igodo runtime tunnel zuru ụwa ọnụ; etinyekwala ya mgbe ọ bụla na ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                       | Aha kpọmkwem nke connector omenala ChatGPT e mepụtara ọhụrụ maka njikọ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ndekọ pụrụ iche maka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị, broker na tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-diagnostics.ts` | Mgbe edobere ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, ọ na-echekwa onyonyo nchọpụta nsogbu ihe nchọgharị n'ebe nlele ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ binary launcher na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na faịlụ binary runtime Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                                   | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; nrụnye ọhụrụ na-eji aha ọkọlọtọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

---

## Àkwà Mmiri OmniConductor

Onye nnata SSE na-adịte aka nke na-egosipụta ọrụ hub OmniConductor n’ime A2A TaskManager mpaghara (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — àkwà mmiri ahụ na-amalite naanị mgbe edobere `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Ọ bụ naanị maka akụkụ sava: token hub ahụ agaghị eru na ihe nchọgharị ma ọlị.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                 | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL ntọala nke hub OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya = agbanyụrụ àkwà mmiri ahụ.        |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ihe nzere hub maka iyi SSE — mepụta peer nke ụdị `spokesperson` na hub ahụ (`POST /v1/peers`, onye nchịkwa).            |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Ihe nzere maka inyefe ọrụ A2A→hub na-abata (`POST /v1/tasks`); ọ na-alaghachi iji `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` mgbe edoghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL ntọala nke ọrụ spokesperson (Faro) dị n’azụ proxy nkata dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                           |

### Nhazi oge na-eburu oke n’uche

`open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji nke a maka nyocha mmefu token tupu arịrịọ. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — omume routing ndabara anaghị agbanwe mgbe edoghị ya.

| Mgbanwe                            | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, mafere njikọ ndị mmefu token ha n’ime windo ọ bụla (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebụl `provider_quota_state`) na-enweghị ike ịkwụ ụgwọ atụmatụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ tupu izipu ya. Kwe ka ọ gaa n’ihu mgbe ahazighị mmefu ọ bụla.                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(on)_  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Nhazi nzaghachi nke ihe atụmatụ token context-guard (#14931): EMA maka (provider, model, tools present) ọ bụla nke `usage.prompt_tokens` / atụmatụ, nke amachibidoro n’etiti 0.15–2.0, naanị n’ime ebe nchekwa. `off`, `0` ma ọ bụ `false` na-agbanyụ ndekọ na itinye ya n’ọrụ. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false` | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Na-edebanye aha ụzọ onyonyo `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli`. Ọ bụ akara atụmatụ dashboard nwere otu aha ahụ; override DB na-emeri uru a.                                                                                                                                      |
